4. ### **8.4 Occupations (Flavor Layer)**

Occupations are **free additions** that define your profession or current role. They grant **starting gear**, **contacts**, and **roleplay hooks** but no mechanical benefits.

1. #### **Sample Occupations**

**Military**: Soldier, Fleet Officer, SAR Specialist, Mercenary, Combat Pilot

**Criminal**: Smuggler, Fence, Hacker, Assassin, Soul Broker

**Academic**: Professor, Archaeologist, Xenobiologist, Archivist

**Trade**: Merchant, Starship Engineer, Field Medic, Artisan, Pilot

**Spiritual**: Priest, Cultist, Ritekeeper, Oracle, Sanctum Keeper

**Unique**: Tulip Freighter Crew, Soul Broker's Assistant, Dream Trainer, SLD Technician, Reality Anchor Specialist

5. ### **8.5 Customizing Your Past**

Work with your GM to create backgrounds that fit your character and campaign.

1. #### **Custom Basic (5 BP)**
* +1 to one Core Attribute
* 2 Skill Proficiencies
* 1 Minor Origin Trait

  #### **Custom Advanced (10 BP)**

* +2 to one Core Attribute (or +1 to two)
* 2 Skill Proficiencies
* 1 Unique Origin Trait
* One **Power Origin** (requires GM approval)

  3. ## **Section 9: Skills**

  Skills represent your character's trained expertise—hard-won knowledge that transforms raw potential (your Core Attributes) into tangible action. Every skill is tied to one of the **Six Core Attributes**, and your proficiency scales directly with your investment of Build Points or Advancement Points.

1. ### **Core Skill Mechanics**

   1. #### **Rank Costs**

   Purchase skill ranks with **Build Points (BP)** during character creation or **Advancement Points (AP)** during play:

* **Ranks 1–5**: 2 BP/AP per rank
* **Ranks 6–10**: 5 BP/AP per rank

  #### **Skill Check Formula**

  **Total = d20 + Attribute Modifier + Skill Rank + Other Bonuses**

  Compare your total against a **Difficulty Class (DC)** set by the GM or an opponent's **Defense Value (DV)**.

  **Success \& Failure**:

* **Success**: Your total meets or exceeds the DC/DV
* **Failure**: Your total falls short
* **Contested Checks**: When two characters oppose each other directly (Persuasion vs. Insight, Stealth vs. Perception), ties go to the defender

  2. ### **9.1 The Skill List by Core Attribute**

  There are **24 core skills**—four per Core Attribute—covering every conceivable action across the infinite multiverse. You gain initial ranks from your Race and Background, then spend BP to expand your expertise.

1. #### **Authority Skills**

|Skill|Typical Uses|
|-|-|
|**Persuasion**|**Convince allies, negotiate treaties, inspire crowds, rally support**|
|**Deception**|**Bluff guards, forge credentials, misdirect pursuers, create cover stories**|
|**Intimidation**|**Demoralize enemies, coerce informants, assert dominance through threats**|
|**Leadership**|**Command squads, coordinate teams, manage organizations, inspire loyalty**|

2. #### **Force Skills**

|Skill|Typical Uses|
|-|-|
|**Melee Combat**|**Close-quarters fighting with weapons or unarmed techniques**|
|**Ranged Combat**|**Firearms, energy weapons, bows, thrown weapons**|
|**Athletics**|**Climb, jump, swim, grapple, perform physical feats**|
|**Piloting**|**Operate vehicles, starships, mechs, atmospheric craft**|

&#x20;     3. #### \*\*Vitality Skills\*\*


|Skill|Typical Uses|
|-|-|
|**Endurance**|**Resist exhaustion, poison, disease, environmental hazards**|
|**Survival**|**Track, forage, navigate wilderness, endure harsh conditions**|
|**Medicine**|**Diagnose illness, treat injuries, stabilize dying creatures, perform surgery**|
|**Animal Handling**|**Train, calm, ride, or work with creatures and beasts**|

&#x20;        4. #### \*\*Mind Skills\*\*


|Skill|Typical Uses|
|-|-|
|**Arcana**|**Identify magical phenomena, recall mystical lore, understand Powers**|
|**Technology**|**Operate, repair, hack, or design technological systems**|
|**Investigation**|**Search for clues, analyze evidence, solve puzzles, reconstruct events**|
|**Research**|**Study texts, access databases, uncover hidden knowledge**|

&#x20;        5. #### \*\*Substance Skills\*\*


|Skill|Typical Uses|
|-|-|
|**Crafting**|**Create items, repair objects, work with raw materials**|
|**Smithing**|**Forge weapons, armor, and metal objects**|
|**Appraisal**|**Determine value, authenticity, and quality of items**|
|**Engineering**|**Design structures, build machinery, solve technical problems**|

&#x20;        6. #### \*\*Spirit Skills\*\*


|Skill|Typical Uses|
|-|-|
|**Religion**|**Recall divine lore, perform rituals, understand sacred traditions**|
|**Insight**|**Read emotions, detect lies, understand motivations**|
|**Perception**|**Notice details, spot hidden things, remain alert to surroundings**|
|**Mysticism**|**Sense supernatural forces, commune with spirits, read omens**|

&#x20;     3. ### \*\*9.2 Power Origin Skill Requirements\*\*


Every **Power Origin** requires proficiency in a specific skill to effectively channel its cosmic forces. You must have **at least Rank 1** in the required skill to use Powers from that Origin.

|Power Origin|Casting Attribute|Required Skill|
|-|-|-|
|**Martial (Way of Steel)**|**Force or Substance**|**Melee Combat or Ranged Combat**|
|**Eternity Channeling**|**Spirit**|**Religion**|
|**Truth Channeling**|**Mind**|**Investigation**|
|**Shadow Channeling**|**Mind or Spirit**|**Mysticism**|
|**Creation Channeling**|**Vitality or Substance**|**Engineering or Crafting**|
|**Life Channeling**|**Spirit**|**Medicine**|
|**Time Channeling**|**Spirit or Mind**|**Mysticism**|
|**Sanctuary Channeling**|**Spirit**|**Religion**|
|**War Channeling**|**Force**|**Melee Combat or Ranged Combat**|
|**Void Channeling**|**Spirit or Mind**|**Mysticism**|
|**Death Channeling**|**Vitality or Spirit**|**Mysticism**|
|**Liberation Channeling**|**Authority**|**Persuasion**|
|**Dominion Channeling**|**Authority**|**Leadership**|

4. ### **9.3 Skill Synergy \& Mastery**

Skills are not isolated—combining expertise creates powerful advantages, and deep mastery unlocks unique benefits.

1. #### **Skill Synergy Bonus**

When **two skills logically apply to the same action**, gain a **+2 synergy bonus** if you have at least **Rank 3** in the secondary skill.

**Examples**:

* **Athletics + Piloting**: Maneuvering through dangerous terrain while maintaining vehicle control
* **Insight + Persuasion**: Tailoring arguments based on emotional reads
* **Technology + Engineering**: Designing and implementing custom technological solutions

  2. #### **Skill Mastery Milestones**

Every **5 ranks** unlocks a permanent benefit:

|Rank|Mastery Tier|Benefit|
|-|-|-|
|**5**|**Competent**|**NPCs recognize your professional skill and may seek your expertise**|
|**10**|**Expert**|**Once per session, gain Advantage on one check with this skill**|
|**15**|**Master**|**Your reputation spans star systems. Attempt tasks others consider impossible (DC 30+)**|
|**20+**|**Legendary**|**Work with GM to create a unique signature ability tied to this skill**|

5. ### **9.4 Custom Skills**

If your character concept requires a specialized skill not listed, work with your GM:

**1. Assign a Core Attribute**: Tie it to one of the Six Core Attributes

**2. Define Scope**: Specific enough to be useful, not so broad it replaces multiple skills

**3. Set Cost**: Standard BP/AP costs (2 per rank 1–5, 5 per rank 6–10)

1. #### **Example Custom Skills**
* **Soul Link Engineering** (Mind): Repair, modify, or diagnose Soul Link Devices
* **Astral Navigation** (Spirit): Navigate the astral plane and Soulvegr pathways
* **Temporal Analysis** (Mind): Detect timeline anomalies and temporal disturbances
* **Divine Politics** (Authority): Navigate the complex hierarchies of the 12 Houses

  4. ## **Section 10: Talents (Innate Gifts)**

Talents are **exceptional abilities** that mark you as touched by cosmic forces. While not reality-warping powers, they represent profound gifts that set you apart from ordinary mortals and hint at your potential for greatness. A Talent defines a signature aspect of your hero's legend.

1. ### **10.1 Choosing Your Talent**

**At Character Creation**: You may purchase **one Talent** for **30 Build Points (BP)**.

**During Play**: Additional Talents can be acquired through:

* **Advancement Points (AP)**: Purchase for **30 AP** (requires GM approval)
* **Narrative Milestones**: Awarded by GM for exceptional achievements

**Limit**: Most Initiate characters will possess **1-2 Talents**. More powerful Talents unlock at Advanced and Ascended Epochs.

2. ### **10.2 Initiate Epoch Talents**

Six Talents per Realm, appropriate for Levels 1-10.

3. ### **Eternity Realm Talents**

|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Deathless Resilience**|**Once per Long Rest, when reduced to 0 HP, you drop to 1 HP instead. Additionally, you stabilize automatically when dying and have Advantage on death saves.**|
|**Ageless Vigor**|**You age at 1/4 normal rate and appear to be in your physical prime. You are immune to magical aging effects and have Advantage on saves against Exhaustion.**|
|**Perfect Recall**|**You have flawless memory of everything you've personally experienced. You can recall exact conversations, visual details, or sensory information from any point in your life. Gain +3 to all Research and Investigation checks involving your own memories.**|
|**Unbreakable Oath**|**When you swear a formal oath, you gain +2 to all rolls directly related to fulfilling it. If you knowingly break the oath, you lose all benefits of this Talent for 1 month.**|
|**Persistent Existence**|**You have Advantage on saves against effects that would remove you from existence, banish you, or erase you from time. You remember events even when timelines are altered (though you cannot prevent the changes).**|
|**Eternal Watcher**|**You need only 4 hours of rest for Long Rest benefits. You cannot be magically put to sleep against your will.**|

4. ### **Truth Realm Talents**

|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Piercing Gaze**|**You have Advantage on Insight checks to detect lies. Once per Short Rest, you can ask a creature one question they must answer truthfully (they know they're compelled but cannot resist—DC 15 Spirit save to resist).**|
|**Truth Speaker**|**Creatures have Advantage on checks to believe you when you speak the truth. However, you take 1d6 psychic damage whenever you speak a deliberate lie.**|
|**Revelation**|**Once per Long Rest, you can spend 10 minutes in meditation to receive a cryptic but truthful answer to one question about your current situation from the GM.**|
|**See Through Deception**|**You automatically detect illusions within 30 feet (though you don't necessarily know what the truth is). Shapechangers appear outlined in faint light visible only to you.**|
|**Inevitable Discovery**|**Once per session, when searching for a specific piece of information or clue, you can declare "I find it." The GM must provide you with a relevant clue, hint, or piece of evidence.**|
|**Scholar's Mind**|**You learn languages in 1/4 the normal time. You are considered to have Rank 3 proficiency in all Knowledge skills you don't already have ranks in.**|

&#x20;     5. ### \*\*Shadow Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Shadow Step**|**Once per Short Rest, you can teleport up to 60 feet from one area of dim light or darkness to another as a Bonus Action.**|
|**One with Darkness**|**When in dim light or darkness, you have Advantage on Stealth checks and can attempt to Hide even while being observed.**|
|**Shadow Cloak**|**As a Bonus Action, you can extinguish one non-magical light source within 30 feet. Additionally, you can create a 10-foot radius area of magical darkness centered on yourself (lasts 1 minute, once per Short Rest).**|
|**Unseen Movement**|**You make no sound when you move. You have Advantage on Stealth checks to move quietly regardless of terrain.**|
|**Dark Vision**|**You can see normally in magical and natural darkness out to 120 feet.**|
|**Shadow Sense**|**You can sense the presence and general location of all creatures within 30 feet while you are in darkness, even if you cannot see them.**|

&#x20;     6. ### \*\*Creation Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Masterwork Crafting**|**Items you personally craft gain +1 quality bonus. Crafting time is reduced by 25%. Your crafted items are obviously superior to standard versions.**|
|**Mending Touch**|**You can repair damaged non-magical objects by touching them and spending 1 minute concentrating. You can restore up to 50 HP worth of object damage per Long Rest this way.**|
|**Material Insight**|**By touching any object, you instantly know what it's made of, how it was constructed, and its approximate age. You have Advantage on Appraisal checks.**|
|**Quick Construction**|**You can craft simple items (tools, weapons, basic equipment) in half the normal time if you have appropriate materials available.**|
|**Improvised Genius**|**Once per Short Rest, you can create a makeshift tool or simple device from available materials. It functions for 1 hour before falling apart.**|
|**Reshape Matter**|**Once per Long Rest, you can alter the shape (but not material) of one non-magical object you touch. You could turn a sword into a dagger, reshape a bar into a key, etc. The object must remain roughly the same mass.**|

&#x20;     7. ### \*\*Life Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Rapid Regeneration**|**At the start of your turn, if you have at least 1 HP, you regain HP equal to your Vitality modifier (minimum 1).**|
|**Healer's Gift**|**When you use Medicine to stabilize a dying creature, they regain 1 HP. You can add your Vitality modifier to all healing you provide (spells, Medicine checks, Powers).**|
|**Hardy Constitution**|**Your maximum HP increases by +15. You have Advantage on saves against disease and poison.**|
|**Vital Surge**|**Once per Long Rest, as a Bonus Action, you can heal yourself for HP equal to 3× your level.**|
|**Life Sense**|**You can sense the presence of living creatures within 60 feet, even through walls (you don't know exact locations, just general direction and number).**|
|**Poison Immunity**|**You are completely immune to non-magical poisons. You have Advantage on saves against magical poisons and take half damage from poison damage.**|

&#x20;     8. ### \*\*Time Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Temporal Dodge**|**Once per Short Rest, when you would be hit by an attack, you can use your Reaction to reroll the attacker's attack roll and take the new result.**|
|**Perfect Timing**|**You gain +5 to Initiative rolls and cannot be Surprised. You always know the exact current time.**|
|**Déjà Vu**|**Once per Long Rest, you can reroll any d20 you just rolled (attack, save, or check) and take the new result.**|
|**Temporal Awareness**|**Once per session, you can ask the GM "What will happen if I do this?" before taking an action. The GM must give you a brief, honest answer.**|
|**Haste**|**Once per Long Rest, you can take an additional Action on your turn (this does not grant another Bonus Action or Reaction).**|
|**Time Sense**|**You can sense when time is being magically manipulated within 60 feet. You have Advantage on saves against time-altering effects.**|

&#x20;     9. ### \*\*Sanctuary Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Guardian's Aura**|**Allies within 10 feet of you gain +1 to all saving throws.**|
|**Protective Sacrifice**|**When an ally within 30 feet would take damage, you can use your Reaction to take that damage instead.**|
|**Sacred Ground**|**You can spend 10 minutes to consecrate a 30-foot radius area. While within this area, you and your allies have Advantage on saves against Fear and undead/fiends have Disadvantage on attacks. The consecration lasts 8 hours. You can maintain one at a time.**|
|**Inviolate Mind**|**You have Advantage on saves against Charm and Possession effects. Your thoughts cannot be read without your permission.**|
|**Warding Presence**|**Once per Short Rest, you can grant one ally within 30 feet Resistance to all damage until the end of your next turn.**|
|**Sanctuary**|**You can designate one location per month as your Sanctuary (requires 1 hour ritual). While in your Sanctuary, you have Advantage on all saves and gain +2 to Defense Value.**|

&#x20;     10. ### \*\*War Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Combat Superiority**|**You gain +2 to all Melee Combat and Ranged Combat attack rolls.**|
|**Battle Fury**|**Once per Short Rest, when you reduce a creature to 0 HP, you can immediately make one additional attack against another creature within range as a Bonus Action.**|
|**Weapon Bond**|**Choose one specific weapon. You always know its location within 1 mile and can summon it to your hand as a Bonus Action if it's within 60 feet. You cannot be disarmed of this weapon.**|
|**Tactical Mind**|**You gain +3 to Initiative. Once per combat, you can grant an ally within 60 feet Advantage on their next attack roll.**|
|**Relentless**|**Once per Long Rest, when reduced to 0 HP, you can make one full Attack action before falling unconscious.**|
|**War Cry**|**Once per Short Rest, as a Bonus Action, you can let out a fearsome war cry. All enemies within 30 feet must succeed on a DC 13 Spirit save or become Frightened of you until the end of your next turn.**|

&#x20;     11. ### \*\*Void Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Void Sight**|**You can see invisible and ethereal creatures within 60 feet. You can see normally in magical darkness.**|
|**Entropy Touch**|**Once per Short Rest, your melee attacks deal an additional 2d6 necrotic damage for 1 minute.**|
|**Void Resistance**|**You have Resistance to necrotic damage. You have Advantage on saves against Void-based effects.**|
|**Nullify Magic**|**Once per Long Rest, when you are targeted by a spell or magical effect, you can use your Reaction to attempt to nullify it (the caster must make a DC 13 Spirit save or the effect fails).**|
|**Embrace the Void**|**You can survive in vacuum or airless environments for 10 minutes without harm. You have Advantage on saves against suffocation.**|
|**Entropic Aura**|**Non-magical objects you touch for more than 1 minute begin to deteriorate. You can deliberately accelerate this to destroy an object over 10 minutes.**|

&#x20;     12. ### \*\*Death Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Death Ward**|**Once per Long Rest, when you would be killed outright (reduced to negative HP equal to your maximum), you instead drop to 1 HP.**|
|**Grave Sense**|**You can sense undead and corpses within 60 feet. By touching a corpse, you learn how they died.**|
|**Soul Sight**|**You can see the soul grades of living creatures as colored auras. You automatically detect invisible undead within 30 feet.**|
|**Speak with Dead**|**Once per Long Rest, you can speak with a corpse that has been dead less than 7 days. You can ask up to 3 questions. The corpse is not compelled to be truthful.**|
|**Necrotic Resistance**|**You have Resistance to necrotic damage. You are immune to effects that would raise you as undead against your will.**|
|**Life Drain**|**Once per Short Rest, when you deal damage to a living creature with an attack, you regain HP equal to half the damage dealt.**|

&#x20;     13. ### \*\*Liberation Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Escape Artist**|**You have Advantage on checks to escape grapples, bonds, or restraints. You can use a Bonus Action to attempt to escape rather than using an Action.**|
|**Unshackled Mind**|**You have Advantage on saves against Charm effects. Effects that would dominate or control your actions last half as long (minimum 1 round).**|
|**Freedom's Call**|**Once per Long Rest, you can end one ongoing Charm, Fear, or Restraint effect on yourself or a creature you touch.**|
|**Untraceable**|**Magical tracking and scrying effects targeting you have Disadvantage. You leave no tracks unless you choose to.**|
|**Liberator**|**Once per Short Rest, you can grant one ally within 30 feet Advantage on their next save against a Charm, Fear, or Domination effect.**|
|**Slip Away**|**Once per Short Rest, when you would be grappled or restrained, you can use your Reaction to automatically avoid the effect.**|

&#x20;     14. ### \*\*Dominion Realm Talents\*\*


|Talent|Effect|
|-|-|
|**Commanding Presence**|**You have Advantage on Intimidation and Leadership checks. Lower-level NPCs (commoners, basic soldiers) treat you with instinctive respect.**|
|**Voice of Authority**|**Once per Short Rest, you can issue a one-word command (Approach, Flee, Halt, Drop, etc.) to one creature within 30 feet. They must succeed on a DC 13 Spirit save or obey on their next turn.**|
|**Tactical Commander**|**Allies who can hear you gain +1 to attack rolls on their first attack each round.**|
|**Intimidating Aura**|**Once per Short Rest, you can force all hostile creatures within 30 feet to make a DC 13 Spirit save or become Frightened of you for 1 minute.**|
|**Born Leader**|**You have Advantage on all Persuasion checks when giving orders or organizing groups. You can coordinate up to 10 people for complex tasks without penalty.**|
|**Demand Attention**|**When you speak in a commanding voice, all creatures within 60 feet who can hear you must make a DC 13 Spirit save or stop what they're doing and listen for 1 round. This does not prevent them from defending themselves. Once per Short Rest.**|

&#x20;     15. ### \*\*10.3 Custom Talent Framework\*\*


Work with your GM to create unique Talents appropriate for the Initiate Epoch.

**Requirements**:

* Must be **passive or usable limited times per rest**
* Should feel **special and defining** but not overwhelming
* Comparable in power to existing Initiate Talents

**Cost**: 30 BP at creation, or 30 AP during play

5. ## **Section 11: Perks (Advantages of Body, Mind, and Soul)**

Perks are specialized advantages that represent your character's unique training, natural gifts, or cosmic alignments. Unlike Talents (which are profound innate abilities), Perks provide focused bonuses that define how you operate in specific situations—the small edges that keep you alive and thriving across the multiverse.

1. ### **Core Rules for Perks**

**Always Active**: Perks do not require Energy Points (EP) to use.

**Stacking**: You can purchase as many Perks as you can afford, but you cannot purchase the same Perk twice unless explicitly stated.

**No Prerequisites**: Any character can purchase any Perk, regardless of their Race, Background, or Power Origin.

2. ### **Perk Tiers \& Costs**

|Tier|BP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|
|**Minor**|**5 BP**|**Small bonus (+1 or +2), niche advantage, or situational benefit**|
|**Standard**|**10 BP**|**Moderate bonus, significant utility, or broad advantage**|

6. ## **Perks by Realm of Influence**

The following Perks are organized by their thematic connection to the **12 Realms of Influence**. Each Realm reflects a fundamental aspect of reality, and these Perks channel those cosmic forces in practical ways.

1. ### **Eternity Realm Perks**

*The Realm of timelessness, endurance, and unbreakable will*

13. ### **11.2 Custom Perks**

If your character concept requires a Perk not listed here, work with your GM to create one that matches the power level and themes of existing Perks.

**Custom Minor Perk (5 BP)**:

* +1 or +2 bonus to specific types of checks
* Advantage in very narrow situations
* Minor flavor convenience or utility

**Custom Standard Perk (10 BP)**:

* Significant mechanical benefit
* Advantage on broad category of checks
* Unique utility that solves common problems

  7. ## **Section 12: Hindrances (Flaws)**

Hindrances are deep-seated flaws, cosmic scars, or burdens that define your character's struggle. They are not mere inconveniences—they must be actively roleplayed and create real challenges. In exchange, they grant additional Build Points to spend on your hero's strengths.

1. ### **Core Rules for Hindrances**

**Must Be Played**: If you consistently ignore a Hindrance, the GM may revoke the BP gained or impose narrative consequences.

**Maximum Gain**: You may gain up to **+30 BP** total from Hindrances at character creation.

**Creation Only**: Hindrances are chosen during character creation. Some may be removed or reduced through epic quests, costly rituals, or therapy (costs AP equal to twice the BP gained).

**Stacking**: You can take multiple Hindrances, but their effects compound—making your hero's journey genuinely more difficult.

2. ### **Hindrance Tiers**

|Tier|BP Gained|Severity|
|-|-|-|
|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**Occasional inconvenience or niche penalty**|
|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**Frequent challenge or significant limitation**|
|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**Severe disability or life-altering burden**|

8. ## **Hindrances by Realm of Influence**

   1. ### **Eternity Realm Hindrances**

*Cursed by time, trapped in cycles, or carrying eternal burdens*

|Hindrance|Tier|BP Gained|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Eternal Repetition**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**You occasionally (5% chance per day) experience vivid déjà vu that distracts you. Disadvantage on the next check when this occurs.**|
|**Ageless Watcher**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**You've lived longer than you should. Your memories are fractured—Disadvantage on Research checks to recall events more than 1 year old.**|
|**Cursed Oath**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**You once broke an oath. Until you atone, you have Disadvantage on all Authority checks and cannot benefit from Inspiration.**|
|**Time-Scarred**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**Your personal timeline is unstable. Once per session, the GM can impose Disadvantage on one roll, declaring "you've already failed this in another timeline."**|
|**Eternal Burden**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You carry a cosmic debt that can never be fully repaid. 25% of all Credits and valuables you acquire vanish mysteriously within 24 hours.**|
|**Timeless Curse**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You cannot die of old age, but you age normally and feel every year. You suffer chronic pain—Disadvantage on Endurance checks.**|

2. ### **Truth Realm Hindrances**

*Unable to deceive, compelled to reveal, or cursed with unwanted knowledge*

|Hindrance|Tier|BP Gained|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Compulsive Truth-Teller**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**When directly asked a question, you must answer truthfully unless you succeed on a DC 15 Spirit save.**|
|**Burdened by Truth**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**You know a terrible truth that haunts you (GM determines what). Once per session, this knowledge surfaces, giving you Disadvantage on your next check as you're distracted.**|
|**Cannot Deceive**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You are completely incapable of lying, misdirecting, or withholding information when directly questioned. Deception checks automatically fail.**|
|**Cursed Knowledge**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You know something that powerful entities want suppressed. Assassins and agents hunt you. Once per session, the GM introduces a complication from those pursuing you.**|
|**Truth Vision**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You see through all deception automatically, but cannot turn it off. You know everyone's lies, hidden motives, and true feelings—it's psychologically exhausting. Disadvantage on social checks due to social awkwardness.**|

|**Living Darkness**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**Shadows cling to you unnaturally. You cannot benefit from Stealth in bright light, and bright light deals 1d6 radiant damage per round of exposure.**|

*Marked for death, cursed with undeath, or surrounded by decay*

|Hindrance|Tier|BP Gained|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Death Marked**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**Undead can sense your presence within 60 feet. They are drawn to you.**|
|**Pale**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**You look sickly and corpse-like. Disadvantage on Persuasion checks; strangers fear you're diseased.**|
|**Gravespeaker**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**You attract spirits of the dead. Once per session, a ghost or echo manifests near you, potentially revealing your location or causing complications.**|
|**Life Ebbs**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**Death has marked you. When making death saves, you need 4 successes to stabilize instead of 3.**|
|**Undead Beacon**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**Undead are irresistibly drawn to you. Undead encounters are twice as common, and undead have Advantage on attacks against you.**|
|**Half-Dead**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You exist partially in death. Healing Powers restore half HP to you. You appear corpse-like and NPCs react with horror.**|

11. ### **Liberation Realm Hindrances**

*Unable to escape, bound by chains, or compelled to serve*

|Hindrance|Tier|BP Gained|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Indentured**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**You owe service to an organization. They can demand your time occasionally (GM determines when).**|
|**Bound by Word**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**Once you give your word, you cannot break it. Breaking an oath causes 2d10 psychic damage.**|
|**Cannot Flee**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**You cannot take the Disengage action or voluntarily retreat from combat until an ally has already withdrawn.**|
|**Magically Bound**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**A geas or curse compels you toward a specific goal. You have Disadvantage on actions that directly oppose this compulsion.**|
|**Enslaved**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You wear magical shackles that cannot be removed except by your owner. They can track your location and cause you pain (3d10 psychic damage) if you disobey.**|
|**Soul Debt**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**A powerful entity owns part of your soul. They can summon you once per month, and you must obey or suffer 5d10 psychic damage and Exhaustion.**|

12. ### **Dominion Realm Hindrances**

*Weak-willed, unable to command, or compelled to obey*

|Hindrance|Tier|BP Gained|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Meek**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**Disadvantage on Intimidation and Leadership checks. You cannot project authority effectively.**|
|**Submissive**|**Minor**|**+5 BP**|**When someone of higher social status gives you an order, you must succeed on a DC 12 Spirit save to refuse.**|
|**Weak-Willed**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**Disadvantage on saves vs. Charm and Domination effects. Commands feel compelling.**|
|**Cannot Lead**|**Moderate**|**+10 BP**|**You cannot give orders or command others. Leadership checks automatically fail. Allies do not gain benefits from your tactical suggestions.**|
|**Oathbound**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**You are magically bound to serve a master. You must obey their direct commands or suffer 5d10 psychic damage and one level of Exhaustion.**|
|**Mind Controlled**|**Major**|**+15 BP**|**A powerful entity has partial control over your mind. Once per session, the GM can take control of your character for one action.**|

13. ### **12.2 Custom Hindrances**

Work with your GM to create Hindrances that fit your character's story and the campaign setting.

**Guidelines**:

* **Minor (+5 BP)**: Occasional inconvenience or niche limitation
* **Moderate (+10 BP)**: Frequent penalty or significant behavioral restriction
* **Major (+15 BP)**: Severe disability, life-threatening curse, or fundamental limitation

**The BP gained should reflect how often the Hindrance impacts play and how severely it limits your options.**

9. ## **Section 13: Gear \& Trade**

The multiverse of Soulvegr runs on **Credits (Cr)**—the universal currency accepted everywhere from medieval markets to orbital megacorp banks. Whether you're buying a rusted blade on a frontier world or a military-grade plasma rifle in a Spire, Credits open doors.

**Starting Wealth**: 1,500 Cr (or select a Background Package for starting gear)

1. ### **13.1 Weapons: From Blades to Railguns**

Weapons are your primary tools for survival in a dangerous cosmos. They are defined by **Damage**, **Cost**, **Range** (for ranged weapons), and **Properties**.

1. #### **Attack Rolls**
* **Melee Attack**: d20 + Force Modifier + Melee Combat Rank
* **Ranged Attack**: d20 + Mind Modifier + Ranged Combat Rank

  #### **Damage**

* **Melee Weapons**: Weapon Die + Force Modifier
* **Ranged Weapons**: Weapon Die (no modifier unless specified)

  2. #### **Melee Weapons**

|Weapon|Cost|Damage|Properties|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Combat Knife**|**50 Cr**|**1d4 Piercing**|**Light, Thrown (20/60 ft)**|
|**Shock Baton**|**150 Cr**|**1d6 Lightning**|**Light, Stun (target DC 12 Vitality save or lose Reaction)**|
|**Longsword**|**200 Cr**|**1d8 Slashing**|**Versatile (1d10 two-handed)**|
|**Vibro-Blade**|**400 Cr**|**1d8 Slashing**|**Versatile (1d10), High-Tech, Ignores 1 point of armor**|
|**Runic Greataxe**|**500 Cr**|**1d12 Slashing**|**Heavy, Two-Handed, Magical (bypasses non-magical resistance)**|
|**Mono-Katana**|**800 Cr**|**1d10 Slashing**|**Finesse, High-Tech, Ignores 2 AC**|
|**Power Maul**|**600 Cr**|**2d6 Bludgeoning**|**Heavy, Two-Handed, Knockback (push target 10 ft on hit)**|
|**Rocket Hammer**|**1,200 Cr**|**1d12 Bludgeoning**|**Heavy, Two-Handed, Explosive Impact (5-ft splash for half damage)**|
|**Energy Staff**|**700 Cr**|**1d6 Radiant**|**Versatile (1d8), Can channel Powers, Magical**|
|**Plasma Glaive**|**1,500 Cr**|**2d6 Fire**|**Two-Handed, Reach (10 ft), High-Tech**|
|**Soul-Forged Blade**|**3,000 Cr**|**1d10 Slashing**|**Finesse, Magical, Glows in presence of undead/Void creatures**|
|**Neural Whip**|**900 Cr**|**1d6 Lightning**|**Reach (15 ft), On hit: DC 13 Spirit save or Stunned until end of next turn**|

&#x20;    3. #### \*\*Ranged Weapons\*\*


|Weapon|Cost|Damage|Range|Properties|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|**Shortbow**|**100 Cr**|**1d6 Piercing**|**80/320 ft**|**Two-Handed, Ammunition**|
|**Crossbow**|**150 Cr**|**1d8 Piercing**|**100/400 ft**|**Loading, Two-Handed**|
|**Revolver**|**300 Cr**|**1d8 Piercing**|**40/120 ft**|**Reload (6 shots)**|
|**Auto-Rifle**|**600 Cr**|**1d10 Piercing**|**80/300 ft**|**Two-Handed, Burst Fire (Advantage 1/round)**|
|**Laser Pistol**|**800 Cr**|**1d6 Radiant**|**60/150 ft**|**Light, High-Tech, Infinite Ammo (recharges on Long Rest)**|
|**Plasma Rifle**|**1,500 Cr**|**2d6 Fire**|**100/400 ft**|**Heavy, Two-Handed, Overheat (Natural 1 jams weapon for 1 round)**|
|**Sniper Railgun**|**2,500 Cr**|**2d8 Piercing**|**200/800 ft**|**Heavy, Loading, Penetrating (ignores cover bonuses)**|
|**Void Rifle**|**3,500 Cr**|**2d6 Necrotic**|**120/480 ft**|**Two-Handed, Magical, Soul Damage (targets max HP reduced by damage until Long Rest)**|
|**Arc Cannon**|**4,000 Cr**|**3d6 Lightning**|**60/200 ft**|**Heavy, Two-Handed, Chain Lightning (bounces to 1 additional target within 20 ft)**|
|**Graviton Launcher**|**5,000 Cr**|**2d10 Force**|**100/300 ft**|**Heavy, Loading, Gravity Well (creates difficult terrain in 10-ft radius)**|
|**Hand Cannon**|**400 Cr**|**2d6 Piercing**|**30/90 ft**|**Reload (2 shots), Loud, Knockback**|
|**Needle Rifle**|**1,200 Cr**|**1d8 Piercing**|**100/400 ft**|**Silent, Poison Delivery (can coat needles with toxins)**|

&#x20;       4. #### \*\*Weapon Properties Explained\*\*


* **Light**: Can be dual-wielded without penalty
* **Finesse**: Use Force or Mind modifier for attack and damage
* **Versatile**: Can be used one or two-handed (damage increases)
* **Heavy**: Requires Strength 13+ to wield effectively
* **Two-Handed**: Requires both hands to use
* **Loading**: Can only fire once per turn
* **Thrown**: Can be thrown at the range listed
* **Reach**: Extends melee range to specified distance
* **High-Tech**: Requires basic technological knowledge; can malfunction
* **Magical**: Counts as magical for overcoming resistances
* **Ammunition**: Requires ammo (costs vary by type)

  2. ### **13.2 Frames \& Vessels: Bodies of Flesh and Steel**

Your **Vessel** or **Frame** is the physical body your Soul inhabits. While most heroes begin with their natural-born body, technology allows for augmentation, replacement, or complete body-swapping in advanced civilizations.

1. #### **Natural Vessels (0 BP)**

|Vessel Type|Properties|
|-|-|
|**Flesh-Born**|**Your original organic body. No special properties, but no maintenance costs.**|
|**Augmented Natural**|**Minor cybernetic enhancements (cosmetic or basic function). Standard organic vessel with small tech additions.**|

2. #### **Synthetic Frames**

Advanced civilizations can transfer consciousness to synthetic or heavily modified bodies.

|Frame Type|Cost|HP Bonus|Special Properties|Drawbacks|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|**Labor Frame**|**800 Cr**|**+5 HP**|**+2 Force for carrying/lifting, Tireless (need no sleep)**|**-2 Authority (obviously artificial)**|
|**Infiltrator Frame**|**1,500 Cr**|**—**|**+2 Authority (lifelike), Built-in disguise kit**|**Fragile (-5 HP)**|
|**Combat Chassis**|**3,000 Cr**|**+10 HP**|**+1 DV, Built-in weapon mount**|**Obvious (cannot blend in civilian settings)**|
|**Scout Frame**|**1,200 Cr**|**—**|**+10 ft Speed, Enhanced sensors (+2 Perception)**|**Light frame (-5 HP)**|
|**Hazard Frame**|**2,500 Cr**|**+5 HP**|**Vacuum sealed, Radiation shielded, Temperature resistant**|**Heavy (-5 ft Speed)**|
|**Luxury Frame**|**5,000 Cr**|**—**|**+3 Authority, Perfect appearance, Built-in entertainment suite**|**High maintenance (500 Cr/month)**|
|**War Frame**|**8,000 Cr**|**+15 HP**|**+2 DV, +2 Force, Weapon hardpoints**|**Requires Pilot license, Obvious military tech**|
|**Stealth Frame**|**6,000 Cr**|**—**|**+5 to Stealth, Optical camouflage (3/day), Silent movement**|**Fragile (-10 HP)**|

**Frame Maintenance: Synthetic frames require regular maintenance (10% of purchase cost per year) or suffer -1 to all physical rolls.**

3. ### **13.3 Soul Link Devices**

In technologically advanced regions, **Soul Link Devices (SLDs)** replace natural astral cords, allowing for body-swapping, soul backup, and enhanced metaphysical capabilities.

|SLD Type|Cost|Survival DC|Relink Limit|Special Properties|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|**Civic Flicker**|**0 Cr (BP)**|**15**|**3-5**|**Standard civilian issue. Fragile but functional.**|
|**Black Market Rig**|**500 Cr**|**18**|**3-5**|**+1 to Technology checks, Unstable (5% malfunction chance in stress)**|
|**Corporate Standard**|**1,500 Cr**|**12**|**8-10**|**Reliable, backed by warranty, but corp tracks your location**|
|**Military Grade**|**3,000 Cr**|**10**|**10-12**|**Rugged, encrypted, backup protocols**|
|**House-Blessed**|**5,000 Cr**|**8**|**15+**|**Divine House backing, includes minor blessing (+1 to saves)**|
|**Custom Artisan**|**8,000 Cr**|**10**|**12-15**|**Built to specification, can include custom features (GM approval)**|
|**Void-Touched**|**2,000 Cr**|**16**|**5-8**|**Grants Void Sight (60 ft), but attracts Void entities**|
|**Sanctified Core**|**10,000 Cr**|**6**|**20+**|**Blessed by Sanctuary Realm, near-indestructible**|

**Survival DC: When your Vessel reaches 0 HP, roll d20 + Vitality vs. this DC. Success = SLD intact. Failure = True Death.**

**Relink Limit**: Number of times you can transfer to a new Vessel before the SLD degrades and fails.

7. ### **13.7 The Galactic Economy: Credits \& Black Markets**

   1. #### **Standard Trade**

Credits are universally accepted, but their value varies:

* **Primitive Worlds**: 1 Cr = a week's wages for a laborer
* **Standard Worlds**: 1 Cr = a decent meal
* **Advanced Spires**: 1 Cr = a cup of coffee

  #### **Black Markets**

  Certain items cannot be legally purchased and must be acquired through:

* **Contacts**: Use Streetwise or Persuasion to find dealers
* **Faction Favors**: Trade services for restricted gear
* **Theft**: High-risk acquisition
* **Crafting**: Make it yourself if you have the skills

  **Common Black Market Items**:

* Military-grade weapons and armor
* Restricted cybernetics
* Illegal SLD modifications
* Stolen Soul Link Devices
* Void artifacts
* Unregistered starships

  8. ### **13.8 Crafting Materials \& Divine Crystals**

  High-tier gear requires rare materials found through adventure. The most prized are **Divine Crystals**—naturally occurring crystalline formations that embody the 12 Realms of Influence.

1. #### **Exotic Crafting Components**

|**Echo Residue**|**Very Rare**|**Temporal technology**|**Time anomalies, ancient ruins**|

|**Eternal Shard**|**Legendary**|**Ascension items**|**Eternal Throne fragments**|
|**Living Metal**|**Rare**|**Self-repairing equipment**|**Primal worlds, deep-core mining**|

|**Psionite**|**Rare**|**Psionic amplifiers**|**Phrenic homeworlds**|

2. #### **Divine Crystals**

Divine Crystals are found in locations resonant with their Realm and cannot be purchased. Each crystal type grants unique properties when incorporated into crafted items.

|-|-|-|-|-|

|**Veritite (Truth)**|**Clear with golden light**|**+1d6 radiant vs. shapechangers, reveals true forms**|**Auto-detect lies within 10 ft**|**Courts of law, Truth-Seeker monasteries**|

|**Vitastone (Life)**|**Pulsing crimson**|**+1d6 damage, heal wielder for half**|**+10 max HP, auto-stabilize at 0 HP**|**Healing springs, sacred groves**|
|**Temporalite (Time)**|**Shifting silver-blue**|**Target loses Reaction, skip turn on crit**|**Reroll attacker's hit 1/Long Rest**|**Paradox zones, Chrono-Labs**|
|**Aegisite (Sanctuary)**|**Silver-blue shimmer**|**+1d6 radiant, banish extraplanar on crit**|**+2 to all saves, create sanctuary 1/Long Rest**|**Temples, Divine House sanctuaries**|
|**Conflictite (War)**|**Blood red with silver**|**+2 attack/damage, bonus attack on crit**|**+1 AC, DR 2 vs. physical, immune to Fear**|**Ancient battlefields, war memorials**|
|**Nihilite (Void)**|**Lightless black**|**+1d6 necrotic, reduces max HP**|**Resistance to necrotic, 10% magic malfunction**|**Void rifts, corrupted zones**|
|**Mortisyte (Death)**|**Bone white**|**+1d6 necrotic, +2d6 vs. wounded, death save on crit**|**Death Ward 1/Long Rest, see ghosts**|**Mass graves, burial grounds**|
|**Liberite (Liberation)**|**Gold with blue lightning**|**Ignores armor, +2d6 vs. restrained, sever bonds**|**Cannot be restrained/paralyzed/petrified**|**Sites of successful revolutions**|
|**Sovereignite (Dominion)**|**Royal purple with gold**|**Command on hit (DC 13), +1d6 psychic to resisters**|**+2 Authority, NPCs cannot attack without save**|**Throne rooms, seats of power**|

**Crafting with Divine Crystals:**

* **Requirement**: Rank 5+ in Crafting/Smithing/Engineering
* **Time**: 1 week per crystal
* **Check**: DC 18 (Substance + Skill)
* **Effect**: Item gains ONE ability (Offensive OR Defensive) from the crystal

  3. #### **Soul Artifacts**

Soul Artifacts are items infused with soul energy. They cannot be purchased—only found, earned, or created through specific rituals.

|Artifact|Rarity|Effect|Source|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Soul Shard (Ember)**|**Uncommon**|**+5 temporary EP (until used)**|**Willing donation**|
|**Soul Shard (Spark)**|**Rare**|**+10 temporary EP OR +1 Attribute for 24 hours**|**Heroic sacrifice**|
|**Memory Crystal**|**Rare**|**Stores 1 hour of perfect sensory memory**|**Voluntary recording**|
|**Backup Core**|**Very Rare**|**Auto-creates soul backup weekly; resurrect in 7 days if killed**|**Advanced technology + willing soul**|
|**Soul Anchor**|**Legendary**|**Soul cannot be stolen or separated against will**|**Sacred ritual**|
|**Ascension Shard**|**Legendary**|**+2 to one Attribute permanently (max 30), one-time use**|**Epoch breakthrough remnant**|
|**Skill Echo**|**Rare**|**Grants Rank 3 in one skill while held**|**Master's final work**|
|**Battle Echo**|**Rare**|**Weapon grants +1 attack, teaches combat technique**|**Legendary warrior's weapon**|

**Note: Harvesting souls against their will is illegal and evil. Such "forbidden artifacts" exist on black markets but carry severe moral and legal consequences.**

10. ## **Section 14: Powers System**

The unified system for all active abilities—martial techniques, divine miracles, reality manipulation, and cosmic channeling.

1. ### **14.1 Powers Overview**

Powers are all active abilities that cost Energy (EP). This includes martial techniques, divine miracles, reality warping, temporal manipulation, and all other supernatural capabilities.

1. #### **Core Principles**

**Unified System**: There are no separate spell lists. Everything uses the same mechanics regardless of whether you're using martial techniques or channeling cosmic forces.

**Two Types of Origins**:

* **Martial Channeling**: Free to all characters. Represents physical training and discipline.
* **Realm Channeling**: Requires a Channeling Origin (granted by Tier 3 Race, Advanced Background, or purchased for 10 BP). Represents cosmic power.

**Tiered Power**: Powers come in three tiers. Higher tiers cost more BP/EP but deliver dramatically stronger effects.

**Defense Trumps Offense**: Many defensive Powers automatically succeed against equal-tier offensive Powers. Attackers must outclass defenders to guarantee success.

2. #### **Key Formulas**

**Casting Check** (when needed): d20 + Casting Attribute Modifier + Casting Skill Rank

**Save DC**: 10 + Caster's Attribute Modifier + Caster's Skill Rank

**Heightening**: When you gain a level, you may upgrade one known Power. Options include: increase damage/healing by +1d6, increase range/duration by 50%, or add one additional target.

2. ### **14.2 The Thirteen Power Origins**

   1. #### **Universal Origin (Free to All)**

|Origin|Casting Attribute|Casting Skill|Theme|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Martial Channeling**|**Force**|**Melee/Ranged Combat**|**Physical training, combat technique, tactical mastery**|

&#x20;     2. #### \*\*The Twelve Realm Channeling Origins\*\*


You must have a **Channeling Origin** to purchase Powers from these lists. Origins are granted by certain Tier 3 Races or Advanced Backgrounds, or can be purchased for **10 BP** at character creation.

|Origin|Realm|Casting Attribute|Casting Skill|Theme|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|**War Channeling**|**War**|**Force**|**Melee/Ranged Combat**|**Supernatural combat, battlefield dominance, warrior's fury**|
|**Eternity Channeling**|**Eternity**|**Spirit**|**Religion**|**Timelessness, unbreakable oaths, endurance, preservation**|
|**Truth Channeling**|**Truth**|**Mind**|**Investigation**|**Revelation, forcing honesty, piercing all deception**|
|**Shadow Channeling**|**Shadow**|**Mind or Spirit**|**Mysticism**|**Darkness, stealth, secrets, moving unseen**|
|**Creation Channeling**|**Creation**|**Substance or Vitality**|**Crafting/Engineering**|**Matter manipulation, fabrication, transmutation**|
|**Life Channeling**|**Life**|**Spirit**|**Medicine**|**Healing, vitality, natural connection, growth**|
|**Time Channeling**|**Time**|**Spirit or Mind**|**Mysticism**|**Temporal manipulation, precognition, causality control**|
|**Sanctuary Channeling**|**Sanctuary**|**Spirit**|**Religion**|**Divine protection, sacred ground, refuge**|
|**Void Channeling**|**Void**|**Spirit or Mind**|**Mysticism**|**Entropy, anti-magic, emptiness, nullification**|
|**Death Channeling**|**Death**|**Vitality or Spirit**|**Arcana**|**Necromancy, decay, undead mastery, endings**|
|**Liberation Channeling**|**Liberation**|**Authority**|**Persuasion**|**Freedom, breaking bonds, rebellion, escape**|
|**Dominion Channeling**|**Dominion**|**Authority**|**Leadership**|**Control, command, authority, subjugation**|

3. ### **14.3 Power Mechanics**

   1. #### **Action Economy**
* **Action**: Most Powers require 1 Action to use
* **Bonus Action**: Some Powers are faster (marked "Bonus Action")
* **Reaction**: Some Powers trigger in response to an event (marked "Reaction")
* **Ritual**: Powers with the \[Ritual] tag can be cast over 10 minutes for 0 EP, but require uninterrupted focus

  2. #### **Range, Area, and Duration**
* **Range**: Listed per Power (Touch, 30 ft, 60 ft, etc.)
* **Area**: Listed per Power (Cone, Radius, Line, etc.)
* **Duration**: Listed per Power (Instantaneous, 1 minute, Concentration, etc.)

  3. #### **Components**

Most Powers require a free hand (Somatic) and the ability to speak (Verbal). Some require a specific item (Material). Armor or restraints may interfere with these components.

4. #### **Concentration**

Powers marked **\[Concentration]** require ongoing focus. If you take damage while concentrating, you must make a Concentration Check:

**Check**: d20 + Casting Attribute Modifier vs. DC 10 or half the damage taken (whichever is higher)

**Failure**: The Power ends immediately

**Limit**: You can only concentrate on one Power at a time unless you have a Talent that says otherwise

5. #### **Saving Throws**

When a target resists a Power, they make a Saving Throw:

**Save DC**: 10 + Caster's Attribute Modifier + Caster's Skill Rank

**Tie Rule**: If the target's save roll equals the DC exactly, the defender wins (Defense Trumps Offense)

4. ### **14.4 Purchasing Powers**

Powers are purchased with **Build Points (BP)** at creation or **Advancement Points (AP)** during play.

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost (Typical)|Availability|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1 (Base)**|**5 BP**|**2–4 EP**|**Level 1+ (Initiate Epoch)**|
|**Tier 2 (Improved)**|**+10 BP (15 total)**|**4–7 EP**|**Level 5+ (Initiate Epoch)**|
|**Tier 3 (Mastered)**|**+15 BP (30 total)**|**7–12 EP**|**Level 11+ (Advanced Epoch)**|

**Note: You must own a Power at Tier 1 before upgrading to Tier 2, and own Tier 2 before upgrading to Tier 3.**

5. ### **14.5 Power Origins \& Their Lists**

The following sections detail all Powers available for each Origin. Each Power includes:

* Name and Theme
* Tier 1, 2, and 3 Effects (with BP and EP costs)
* Mechanical Tags (Action type, Range, Duration, Concentration, etc.)

|**14.5.H**|**Time Channeling**|**Temporal manipulation, causality**|

11. ## **14.5.A: Martial Channeling (Force) - FREE TO ALL**

*The foundation of physical combat—weapon technique, tactical awareness, and disciplined training available to any dedicated warrior.*

**Primary Attribute**: Force (Power/Agility)  
**Casting Skill**: Melee Combat or Ranged Combat  
**Prerequisite**: None. Available to all characters.

**Philosophy**: Martial Channeling represents what any mortal can achieve through dedication, training, and discipline. These are grounded, practical combat techniques—no cosmic power required. While less flashy than Realm-based Powers, they form the foundation of all martial excellence.

1. ### **1. Precise Strike**

*Targeting a vulnerable point with careful aim.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**On a successful attack, add +1d6 damage.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Add +2d6 damage. You have Advantage on this attack roll.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Add +3d6 damage. This attack automatically hits if the target's DV is 15 or lower.**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Melee or Ranged (Weapon Range)**

2. ### **2. Defensive Stance**

*Using training to deflect incoming attacks.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Reaction. When hit by an attack, gain +2 to DV against that attack, potentially causing it to miss.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Gain +3 to DV. If the attack misses, you can move 5 ft without provoking opportunity attacks.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**6 EP**|**Gain +4 to DV. If the attack misses, you can make an immediate melee attack against the attacker as part of the same Reaction.**|

**Action: Reaction**  
**Trigger: You are hit by an attack**  
**Range: Self**

3. ### **3. Combat Roll**

*A trained maneuver to reposition quickly.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Bonus Action. Move up to 15 ft without provoking opportunity attacks.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**3 EP**|**Move up to 20 ft without provoking opportunity attacks. You can move through enemy-occupied spaces during this movement.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**5 EP**|**Move up to 30 ft without provoking opportunity attacks. You have Advantage on the next attack you make before the end of your turn.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**

4. ### **4. Aimed Shot**

*Taking extra time to line up a perfect shot.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Ignore penalties for cover or long range on one ranged attack.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Ignore all cover and range penalties. You have Advantage on this attack roll.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**6 EP**|**Ignore all penalties. On a hit, add +2d6 damage. You can target a specific body part (leg = reduce Speed by half, arm = drop held item, head = Disadvantage on attacks until end of their next turn).**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Ranged (Weapon Range)**

5. ### **5. Second Wind**

*Drawing on reserves of stamina to push through injury.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Bonus Action. Heal yourself for 1d8 + Vitality Modifier HP.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Heal 2d8 + Vitality Modifier HP. Remove one level of Exhaustion.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**6 EP**|**Heal 3d8 + Vitality Modifier HP. Remove one negative condition: Poisoned, Frightened, or Slowed.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**

6. ### **6. Disarm**

*A trained technique to knock a weapon from an opponent's grasp.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Make a melee attack roll against target's DV. On hit, target must make a Force Save (DC = 10 + Force Mod + Melee Combat Rank) or drop one held item.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**On a successful disarm, you can kick the item up to 15 ft away or catch it yourself if you have a free hand.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**6 EP**|**You can attempt to disarm all creatures within your reach simultaneously. Each makes their own save.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Melee**

*Using your shield as a weapon.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

12. ## **14.5.B: War Channeling (Force) - War Realm**

*Supernatural combat prowess channeled from the War Realm—techniques that transcend mortal limits and reshape battlefields.*

**Primary Attribute**: Force (Power/Dominance)  
**Casting Skill**: Melee Combat or Ranged Combat  
**Prerequisite**: War Channeling Origin Tag + Combat Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: War Channeling draws power directly from the War Realm, one of the 12 Realms of Influence. This is combat elevated beyond mere technique—it's the embodiment of conflict itself flowing through a warrior's soul. These Powers defy physics, shatter limitations, and turn a single warrior into a force of nature on the battlefield.

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Add +5d8 damage. If this attack reduces a target to 0 HP, you may immediately make another melee attack against a different enemy within 15 ft as a free action (you can move to reach them).**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Melee**

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Fortress Stance. For 1 minute, you gain all benefits of Tier 2. Additionally, enemies that start their turn within 10 ft of you or enter that area take 2d6 Force damage from the pressure of your presence.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration]**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Make three additional attacks as a Bonus Action. If at least two hit the same target, that target must make a Vitality Save (DC = 10 + Force Mod + Combat Rank) or be Stunned until the end of your next turn.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Make five additional attacks as a Bonus Action. Each hit deals an additional +1d6 damage.**|

**Action: Bonus Action (after Attack action)**  
**Range: Melee**

*Perceiving the flow of battle with supernatural clarity.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**You and all allies within 30 ft have Advantage on attacks against that target for 1 minute.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Fatal Weakness. You identify a lethal opening. The next attack made against that target by you or an ally within 1 minute is an automatic Critical Hit. This can only affect each target once per combat.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**

13. ## **14.5.C: Eternity Channeling (Spirit) - Eternity Realm**

*Drawing power from the Eternity Realm—timelessness, unbreakable oaths, endurance beyond mortality, and the preservation of what must not fade.*

**Primary Attribute**: Spirit (Willpower/Soul Strength)  
**Casting Skill**: Religion  
**Prerequisite**: Eternity Channeling Origin Tag + Religion Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Eternity Channeling connects you to the Eternity Realm, where time flows differently and oaths carry the weight of cosmic law. These Powers allow you to resist entropy, bind others with unbreakable promises, and endure beyond mortal limits. Masters of Eternity become anchors of stability in a chaotic multiverse.

1. ### **1. Oath of Binding**

*Sealing a promise with cosmic authority.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch a willing creature who makes an oath. If they knowingly break it within 24 hours, they take 3d8 psychic damage and you instantly know they've broken it.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Duration extends to 1 week. Breaking the oath causes 5d8 psychic damage and Disadvantage on all rolls for 24 hours.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Eternal Compact. The oath lasts until fulfilled or released by you. Breaking it causes 8d8 psychic damage, one level of Exhaustion, and a visible mark appears on the oathbreaker (visible to those with soul-sight).**|

**Action: Action (Ritual - 1 minute)**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Save: None (willing participants only)**

2. ### **2. Timeless Form**

*Suspending the effects of time on your body.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**For 8 hours, you do not age, hunger, thirst, or need sleep. You are immune to magical aging effects.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Duration extends to 24 hours. You also gain Advantage on saves against Exhaustion and disease.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Deathless Moment. For 1 hour, you cannot be reduced below 1 HP by any means. When the duration ends, if you are at 1 HP, you fall unconscious and must make death saves normally.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 8 hours / 24 hours / 1 hour**

3. ### **3. Enduring Presence**

*Drawing on reserves of eternal strength.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Gain 15 Temporary HP. While you have any of these Temp HP, you have Advantage on saves vs. Exhaustion.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Gain 25 Temporary HP. You also gain Resistance to one damage type of your choice (Fire, Cold, Lightning, Acid, Necrotic, or Radiant) for 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Eternal Vigor. Gain 40 Temporary HP. While you have any of these Temp HP remaining, you cannot be knocked unconscious, Stunned, or Paralyzed.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**

4. ### **4. Perfect Recall**

*Preserving a moment in flawless detail.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Touch a willing creature. They gain perfect recall of the last 10 minutes. This memory cannot be magically altered or erased.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Memory Crystal. Store up to 1 hour of sensory memory in a crystal or small object. Anyone touching it experiences the memory as if they lived it.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Witness the Past. Touch a location or object and experience up to 1 minute of its history from any point in the last year. You see events as they actually occurred.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch**

5. ### **5. Preservation**

*Preventing decay and destruction.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch an object up to 5 cubic feet in size. It cannot break, rust, rot, or decay for 24 hours.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Instant Repair. Touch a broken non-magical object. It is instantly restored to perfect condition.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Eternal Stasis. Touch an object or willing creature. They are placed in suspended animation—completely preserved and immune to all damage, aging, and effects. They are unconscious and unaware. Duration: until you dismiss it or 1 year passes.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch**

6. ### **6. Unbroken Focus**

*Refusing to be interrupted.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**For 10 minutes, you have Advantage on all Concentration checks. Taking damage does not force a Concentration check unless you take 20+ damage in one hit.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Perfect Continuity. For 1 minute, you automatically succeed on all Concentration checks. You cannot be forced to break concentration by damage, conditions, or forced movement.**|

**Action: Reaction (T1) / Bonus Action (T2-3)**  
**Range: Self**

7. ### **7. Return**

*Bringing back what was lost to time.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**Revitalize. Touch a creature that died within the last minute. They return to life with 1 HP and 1 level of Exhaustion.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**True Resurrection. Touch a creature that died within the last 7 days. They return fully healed with all conditions and curses removed. You gain 2 levels of Exhaustion from this effort.**|

**Range: Touch**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Eternal Curse. On failure, target is cursed. They cannot benefit from magical healing until the curse is removed by high-level magic (requires a Tier 3 Power or similar). The curse is visible as a dark mark to those with soul-sight.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Timeless Observer. For 1 hour, you exist slightly outside normal time. You can see 6 seconds into the past and future simultaneously. You gain +5 to Initiative and can take your turn on any Initiative count you choose each round.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 1 hour**

*Locking your form against alteration.*

|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Eternal Self. For 1 hour, you are immune to all transmutation magic. You cannot be aged, diseased, poisoned, or have your form changed in any way. Your soul cannot be extracted from your body.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 1 hour**

11. ### **11. Beyond Death's Reach**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Deathless Warrior. When reduced to 0 HP, you remain conscious and can act normally for 1 round before making death saves.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Unkillable. For 1 minute, you cannot be reduced below 1 HP by any means (including massive damage). When the effect ends, if you have 1 HP remaining, you fall unconscious but are stable.**|

**Action: Reaction (T1) / Bonus Action (T2-3)**  
**Range: Self**

12. ### **12. Legacy**

*Calling on the strength of those who came before.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Champion of Ages. For 1 minute, you add +2 to all d20 rolls and can reroll any natural 1 once per round.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**

13. ### **13. Frozen Moment**

*Stretching time subjectively.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Extended Thought. You experience 10 minutes of subjective time. You can mentally prepare strategies, recall information, and examine your surroundings in detail.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Time Pause. Time stops for everyone except you for 1 round. You can move freely and interact with objects, but cannot attack creatures or deal damage. Any attacks you set up resolve when time resumes.**|

**Range: Self**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Life Tether. Touch a creature. For 8 hours, if they die, their soul remains anchored to their body for 1 hour, making resurrection much easier.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Eternal Binding. Touch a willing creature. For 1 week, their soul cannot be stolen, trapped, severed from their body, or prevented from returning after death. They have Advantage on all saves against possession and soul manipulation.**|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**Eternal Haven. Create a 30 ft radius zone for 7 days. Time does not pass inside—creatures do not age, hunger, tire, or need sleep. They are invisible and intangible to the outside world (can see out but cannot be seen or affected). Creatures can leave voluntarily at any time.**|

14. ## **14.5.D: Truth Channeling (Mind) - Truth Realm**

*Drawing power from the Truth Realm—revelation, forcing honesty, piercing all deception, and unveiling hidden reality.*

**Primary Attribute**: Mind (Intellect/Perception)  
**Casting Skill**: Investigation  
**Prerequisite**: Truth Channeling Origin Tag + Investigation Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Truth Channeling connects you to the Truth Realm, where all deception crumbles and reality stands naked. These Powers allow you to compel honesty, see through illusions, and reveal what others desperately try to hide. Masters of Truth become living lie detectors and arbiters of what is real.

1. ### **1. Compel Honesty**

*Forcing truthful answers from unwilling lips.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target within 30 ft must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Mind Mod + Investigation Rank). On failure, they must answer one question truthfully. They are aware they are compelled.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**You can ask up to 3 questions. Target has Disadvantage on the save. They cannot remain silent—they must answer.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Zone of Truth. All creatures within 20 ft must answer all questions truthfully for 10 minutes (Spirit Save to resist initially). They cannot speak lies but can remain silent or be evasive within the bounds of truth.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft (T1-2) / 20 ft radius (T3)**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 10 minutes**

2. ### **2. Sense Deception**

*Detecting falsehood automatically.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**For 10 minutes, you automatically know when any creature within 30 ft speaks a deliberate lie (you don't learn what the truth is, only that they lied).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Range increases to 60 ft. You also sense if a creature is under magical compulsion to lie or tell the truth. Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Perfect Truth Sense. For 1 hour, you detect all lies within 100 ft. You also sense the emotional state behind them (fear, malice, confusion, desperation) and whether the speaker believes what they're saying is true.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30-100 ft**  
**Duration: 10 minutes - 1 hour**

3. ### **3. Reveal**

*Stripping away disguises and illusions.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one creature or object within 60 ft. All illusions affecting it are instantly dispelled and you see its true form. Shapechangers appear as they truly are.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Dispel Illusion. Choose a 20 ft radius area within 60 ft. All illusions and invisible creatures within the area are revealed. Duration: 1 minute.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**True Sight. For 10 minutes, you see all things as they truly are within 120 ft. You automatically see through all illusions, shapechangers appear in true form, invisible creatures are visible, and you can see into the Ethereal Plane.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft / 20 ft radius / Self (120 ft)**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 minute / 10 minutes**

4. ### **4. Unmasking**

*Forcing creatures to reveal their nature.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one creature within 60 ft. Make a Mind check vs their Spirit Save DC. On success, you learn their creature type (humanoid, undead, fiend, etc.) and any active disguises they're using.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**You learn their true name, current intentions toward you, and any magical effects currently affecting them.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Total Revelation. You learn everything from Tier 1 and 2, plus their greatest secret, deepest fear, and strongest desire. Target must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Mind Mod + Investigation Rank) or be Stunned for 1 round from the violation.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Save: Spirit (T3 only)**

5. ### **5. Discern Intent**

*Reading motivations and plans.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Target one creature you can see within 30 ft. You learn their current emotional state and whether they intend harm toward you or your allies. Duration: 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**You learn their immediate plans (next 10 minutes) and can sense when they're about to act violently. You cannot be Surprised by that creature. Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Perfect Understanding. You understand their complete motivations, long-term goals, and what they truly value. You have Advantage on all social checks against them. Duration: 24 hours.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 24 hours**

6. ### **6. Inevitable Truth**

*Reality itself enforces honesty.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Mind Mod + Investigation Rank). On failure, for 1 minute, any lie they speak aloud causes them 1d6 psychic damage.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 2d6. Additionally, when they lie, everyone who hears it instinctively knows it's a lie. Duration: 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Truth Curse. For 1 hour, target takes 4d6 psychic damage per lie and cannot use Deception skill. All creatures automatically detect their lies. Target can choose to remain silent instead of lying.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour**

7. ### **7. Piercing Questions**

*Asking questions that cannot be evaded.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Ask one complex question requiring a full answer (not just yes/no). Target has Disadvantage on save and cannot be evasive—they must provide complete, direct answers.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Interrogation. For 10 minutes, target must answer all your questions completely and truthfully (one save to resist at start). They cannot refuse to answer or remain silent.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 10 minutes**

*Sensing and identifying magical effects.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**You sense exact locations and can identify the school/origin of magic (Divine, Arcane, Void, etc.). You can identify magical items and active spells. Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Magical Sight. For 1 hour, you see magical auras automatically within 60 ft. You know what each spell does, who cast it, and approximately when. You can identify cursed items on sight.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30-60 ft**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 1 hour**

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Complete Analysis. You learn everything from Tier 1 and 2, plus its combat tactics and any hidden abilities. All attacks against it have Advantage for 1 minute.**|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Voice of Truth. For 24 hours, your truthful statements cannot be disbelieved or disputed. They are treated as established fact in any negotiation or legal proceeding. Magical lie detection confirms your honesty.**|

**Range: Self**
