|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one creature within 60 ft who has lied to you in the last minute. They take 3d8 psychic damage (Spirit Save for half, DC = 10 + Mind Mod + Investigation Rank).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 5d8. On failed save, target is also Stunned until the end of their next turn as truth overwhelms them.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Divine Retribution. Target takes 8d8 psychic damage. On failed save, they are Stunned for 1 minute (save ends) and marked with a visible symbol of their deception that all can see.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**

15. ### **15. Perfect Knowledge**

*Accessing truth directly from the universe.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**4 EP**|**Ask the GM one yes/no question about something happening in the present within 1 mile. The GM must answer truthfully.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**Ask one complex question about events within the past 24 hours or present circumstances. The GM provides a complete, truthful answer.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**10 EP**|**Cosmic Oracle. Ask any question about past, present, or probable future. The universe provides a truthful, detailed answer. This can reveal hidden plots, true identities, or the location of lost things. Once per session.**|

**Action: Action (Ritual - 10 minutes)**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

15. ## **14.5.E: Shadow Channeling (Mind or Spirit) - Shadow Realm**

*Drawing power from the Shadow Realm—darkness, stealth, secrets, and moving through the spaces where light cannot reach.*

**Primary Attribute**: Mind or Spirit (Player's Choice)  
**Casting Skill**: Mysticism  
**Prerequisite**: Shadow Channeling Origin Tag + Mysticism Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Shadow Channeling connects you to the Shadow Realm, where darkness is not the absence of light but a tangible force. These Powers allow you to move unseen, manipulate darkness, and strike from the places between. Masters of Shadow become living secrets—present but unnoticed, dangerous but unseen.

1. ### **1. Shadow Strike**

*Your attack emerges from darkness itself.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Make one melee or ranged attack. On hit, deal weapon damage +1d8 necrotic damage. If you are in dim light or darkness, you have Advantage on the attack.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to +2d8 necrotic. On hit, create a 5 ft radius area of magical darkness centered on the target for 1 round.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Damage increases to +4d8 necrotic. On hit, target must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Mind/Spirit Mod + Mysticism Rank) or be Blinded for 1 minute (save ends).**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Melee or Ranged (Weapon Range)**

2. ### **2. Step Through Shadow**

*Teleporting through darkness.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Bonus Action. Teleport up to 30 ft to an unoccupied space you can see that is in dim light or darkness.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Range increases to 60 ft. When you arrive, you have Advantage on the first attack you make before the end of your turn.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Shadow Walker. Teleport up to 120 ft to any area of darkness (dim light or darkness) you can see. When you arrive, all enemies within 10 ft must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Mind/Spirit Mod + Mysticism Rank) or be Frightened of you until the end of your next turn.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**

3. ### **3. Cloak of Night**

*Wrapping yourself in supernatural darkness.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**While in dim light or darkness, you have Advantage on Stealth checks and can attempt to Hide even while being observed. Duration: 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**You become invisible while in dim light or darkness. You remain invisible until you attack, cast a Power, or enter bright light. Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Perfect Concealment. For 1 hour, you are invisible in any lighting condition. Even creatures with Darkvision or Truesight have Disadvantage on checks to perceive you.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 1 hour**

4. ### **4. Summon Darkness**

*Creating zones of impenetrable shadow.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Create a 15 ft radius sphere of magical darkness centered on a point within 60 ft. Even Darkvision cannot see through it. Duration: 10 minutes \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Radius increases to 20 ft. You can see normally through the darkness you create. Creatures inside have Disadvantage on attack rolls. Duration: 1 hour \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Absolute Darkness. Create a 30 ft radius sphere. Even creatures with Truesight cannot see through it (except you). Creatures inside are Blinded and have their Speed halved. Duration: 1 hour \[Concentration].**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 1 hour \[Concentration]**

5. ### **5. Shadow Veil**

*Extending your concealment to others.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one willing creature. They gain the benefits of your Cloak of Night Power (if you have it) or Advantage on Stealth checks for 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Affect up to 3 willing creatures within 10 ft. They become invisible while in dim light or darkness for 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Mass Concealment. Affect up to 6 creatures within 20 ft. They become invisible (in any lighting) for 1 hour or until they attack.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch / 10 ft / 20 ft**  
**Duration: 1 hour**

6. ### **6. Living Shadow**

*Transforming into pure darkness.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**4 EP**|**You become a two-dimensional shadow for 1 minute. You can move along walls and floors. You cannot attack but gain Resistance to physical damage.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**6 EP**|**Shadow Form. You become incorporeal darkness for 10 minutes. You are immune to physical damage and can pass through solid objects (but not magical barriers). You can still use Shadow Powers.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**9 EP**|**Perfect Shadow. For 1 hour, you exist as pure shadow. You are immune to all damage except radiant, can pass through any non-magical barrier, and can Hide anywhere there is even the slightest shadow.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour**

7. ### **7. Shadow Binds**

*Chains of solid darkness restrain your foes.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one creature within 60 ft standing in dim light or darkness. They must make a Force Save (DC = 10 + Mind/Spirit Mod + Mysticism Rank) or be Restrained for 1 round.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Constricting Shadows. Target is Restrained for 1 minute (save ends). They take 2d6 necrotic damage at the start of each of their turns while Restrained.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Shadow Prison. Target up to 3 creatures within 30 ft. Each must save or be Paralyzed for 1 minute (save ends). They are wrapped in impenetrable darkness and cannot see or be seen.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft / 30 ft**  
**Save: Force**  
**Duration: 1 round / 1 minute / 1 minute \[Concentration]**

8. ### **8. Drain Light**

*Extinguishing illumination.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Extinguish one non-magical light source (torch, lantern, etc.) within 60 ft as a Bonus Action.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Extinguish all non-magical light sources within 60 ft. Magical light sources are suppressed for 1 minute.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Consume Light. Extinguish all light (magical and non-magical) within 100 ft. For 10 minutes, no new light sources can be created in this area except by Powers of Tier 3 or higher.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: 60-100 ft**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 minute / 10 minutes**

9. ### **9. Umbral Blade**

*A weapon forged from solid shadow.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Create a blade of shadow in your hand. It deals 1d8 + Mind/Spirit Mod necrotic damage. It ignores non-magical armor (target's DV = 10 + Force Mod). Duration: 1 minute.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 2d8 + Mod. On hit, target's Speed is reduced by 10 ft until the end of their next turn. Duration: 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Damage increases to 3d8 + Mod. On hit, target must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Mind/Spirit Mod + Mysticism Rank) or be Blinded for 1 round. The weapon can cut through non-magical material as if it were air. Duration: 1 hour.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self (melee weapon)**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour**

10. ### **10. Shadow Sense**

*Perceiving through darkness.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Gain Darkvision (60 ft) for 1 hour. You can see normally in magical and natural darkness.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Shadow Sight. For 1 hour, you can see through darkness as if it were bright light out to 120 ft. You also sense the presence of all creatures in darkness within 60 ft (even through walls).**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Perfect Dark Vision. For 8 hours, you see perfectly in any darkness out to 200 ft. You can see invisible creatures and detect illusions automatically. Darkness provides no concealment or cover against your attacks.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 1 hour / 1 hour / 8 hours**

11. ### **11. Nightmare**

*Projecting terrifying visions from shadow.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one creature within 30 ft. They must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Mind/Spirit Mod + Mysticism Rank) or be Frightened of you for 1 minute (save ends).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Waking Terror. Target is Frightened and takes 2d8 psychic damage at the start of each of their turns while Frightened. They see shadowy horrors only they can perceive.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Phantasmal Killer. Target takes 4d8 psychic damage at the start of each turn and is Paralyzed with fear for 1 minute (save ends both effects). If reduced to 0 HP by this damage, they die of fright.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration]**

*Creating illusory copies made of darkness.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Mirror Image. Create 3 duplicates. Attacks randomly target one (roll d6: 1-2 hit you, 3-4 first duplicate, 5 second, 6 third). Duration: 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Shadow Legion. Create 5 semi-solid duplicates. Each has 1 HP and can move independently (share your Initiative). They can make melee attacks (1d6 necrotic damage). Duration: 1 hour.**|

**Range: Self**

13. ### **13. Steal Shadow**

*Severing a creature's shadow to weaken them.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Shadow Theft. You steal the target's shadow permanently (until you release it or die). While you possess it, target has Disadvantage on all saves and you can use your Reaction to see through their eyes and hear what they hear for 1 round (unlimited uses).**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Target up to 3 creatures. Each is pulled 20 ft and Restrained if they end in darkness (save ends).**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Shadow Vortex. All creatures within 30 ft of a point of darkness you choose are pulled 30 ft toward it. Those ending within 5 ft of the center are Restrained and take 4d6 necrotic damage. Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration].**|

**Action: Action**

**Save: Force**

*Becoming one with shadow itself.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

You are immune to all damage except radiant, can teleport between any shadows within 120 ft as a Bonus Action (unlimited), pass through any barrier, and your Shadow Powers cost half their normal EP (minimum 1). When this ends, you gain 1 level of Exhaustion.**|

**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour**

16. ## **14.5.F: Creation Channeling (Substance or Vitality) - Creation Realm**

*Drawing power from the Creation Realm—fabrication, transmutation, matter manipulation, and the shaping of reality itself.*

**Primary Attribute**: Substance or Vitality (Player's Choice)  
**Casting Skill**: Crafting or Engineering  
**Prerequisite**: Creation Channeling Origin Tag + Crafting or Engineering Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Creation Channeling connects you to the Creation Realm, where matter bends to will and form follows intention. These Powers allow you to shape, create, and transform the physical world. Masters of Creation become living forges—architects of reality who craft the impossible with their bare hands.

1. ### **1. Instant Fabrication**

*Conjuring tools and weapons from raw energy.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Bonus Action. Create one simple, non-magical tool or weapon in your hand (lasts 10 minutes). It functions normally but appears crafted from solidified light or crystal.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Create a complex tool, weapon, or piece of equipment (including firearms or tech devices). Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Masterwork Creation. Create any non-magical weapon or tool of masterwork quality (+1 to attack/damage or +2 to skill checks using the tool). Duration: 8 hours. At Tier 3, you can choose to make the item permanent by spending an additional 10 EP.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self (in hand)**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 8 hours or Permanent**

2. ### **2. Fortify Matter**

*Reinforcing objects and structures.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch an object or 5 ft section of a structure. It gains +5 HP and becomes harder to break (DC to break increases by 5) for 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Instant Repair. Touch a damaged object or structure. Restore up to 4d8 HP worth of damage to it. If repairing a vehicle or construct, it regains functionality.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Unbreakable. Touch an object or 10 ft section of structure. For 24 hours, it becomes immune to all non-magical damage and gains Resistance to magical damage.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Duration: 1 hour / Instantaneous / 24 hours**

3. ### **3. Reshape**

*Transmuting one material into another.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch up to 5 cubic feet of material. Transform it into another non-precious material of similar value (wood to stone, cloth to leather, etc.). Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Transform up to 20 cubic feet. You can create precious materials (iron to silver, stone to marble). Duration: 8 hours or make permanent with a DC 15 Substance check.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Master Transmutation. Transform up to 50 cubic feet into any material you're familiar with, including rare metals (steel to mithral, stone to gold). Transformation is permanent.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Duration: 1 hour / 8 hours or Permanent / Permanent**

*Creating an animated helper.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**Medium Construct (AC 15, 40 HP, Speed 30 ft). It can perform complex tasks and fight effectively (melee attack 2d6 + 3 damage, or ranged attack 1d8 + 3 damage at 60 ft). Duration: 8 hours.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**War Construct (AC 18, 80 HP, Speed 40 ft, Large size). It makes two attacks per turn (2d8 + 4 damage each), provides half cover to adjacent allies, and can carry 500 lbs. Duration: 24 hours or permanent if you spend an additional 20 EP.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Adjacent space**  
**Duration: 1 hour / 8 hours / 24 hours or Permanent**

5. ### **5. Disintegration Ray**

*Unmaking matter with concentrated energy.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 5d8 Force. If this reduces an object to 0 HP, it disintegrates into dust. If it reduces a construct or creature to 0 HP, they must make a Vitality Save (DC = 10 + Substance/Vitality Mod + Crafting/Engineering Rank) or disintegrate.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Damage increases to 8d8 Force. No save—anything reduced to 0 HP disintegrates completely, including all non-magical equipment. Only divine intervention or Tier 3 resurrection can restore the target.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**

*Reshaping existing objects.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Adamantine Form. Gain 40 Temporary HP, +4 to DV, Resistance to all physical damage, and immunity to critical hits for 1 hour. Your unarmed strikes count as magical and deal 1d8 + Substance/Vitality Mod damage.**|

They take 2d6 Force damage at the start of each turn. Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration].**|

14. ### **14. Melt**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one metal object. It becomes molten hot: anyone holding it takes 2d6 fire damage and drops it. Objects can be reshaped while molten. Duration: 1 minute.**|

Creatures standing on it when it melts must make a Force Save (DC = 10 + Substance/Vitality Mod + Skill Rank) or sink and become Restrained.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Mass Meltdown. All metal objects and structures within 30 ft become molten. Weapons, armor, and constructs take 6d6 fire damage. Metal armor melts off (DV becomes 10 + Force Mod). Structures collapse.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch / 30 ft / 30 ft radius**  
**Save: Force (T2-3)**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / Instantaneous**

15. ### **15. Genesis**

*Creating matter from nothing.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**5 EP**|**Create up to 10 lbs of any mundane, non-precious material from thin air (rope, cloth, food, water, wood, stone, etc.). The material is permanent.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**8 EP**|**Create up to 50 lbs of any material, including precious metals and gems (up to 100 Cr value per use). The material is permanent and genuine.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**Master Creation. Create up to 200 lbs of any material, or a complete non-magical object of masterwork quality (weapon, armor, tool, device) worth up to 1,000 Cr. The object is permanent and fully functional. You can create complex mechanisms and technology if you understand how they work.**|

**Action: Action (T1-2) / 10 minutes (T3)**  
**Range: Adjacent space**  
**Duration: Permanent**

17. ## **14.5.G: Life Channeling (Spirit) - Life Realm**

*Drawing power from the Life Realm—healing, vitality, natural connection, growth, and the preservation of living essence.*

**Primary Attribute**: Spirit (Life Force/Connection)  
**Casting Skill**: Medicine  
**Prerequisite**: Life Channeling Origin Tag + Medicine Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Life Channeling connects you to the Life Realm, where vitality flows like water and growth knows no bounds. These Powers allow you to heal wounds, enhance vigor, commune with nature, and sustain life against all odds. Masters of Life become conduits of pure vitality—healers who can resurrect the dead and druids who command the natural world.

1. ### **1. Vital Restoration**

*Channeling life energy to mend wounds.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one creature. Heal them for 2d8 + Spirit Modifier HP.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Range increases to 30 ft. Heal 4d8 + Spirit Modifier HP. Target also regains one expended Hit Die.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Mass Healing. Heal up to 6 creatures within 30 ft for 4d8 + Spirit Modifier HP each. All targets also remove one level of Exhaustion.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch / 30 ft / 30 ft radius**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

2. ### **2. Regeneration**

*Accelerating natural healing processes.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one creature. They regain 1d8 HP at the start of each of their turns for 1 minute \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Healing increases to 2d8 HP per turn. Target also has Advantage on saves vs. poison and disease. Duration: 10 minutes \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Perfect Regeneration. Target regains 3d8 HP at the start of each turn and can regrow lost limbs over the duration. If reduced to 0 HP, they stabilize automatically. Duration: 1 hour \[Concentration].**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour \[Concentration]**

3. ### **3. Purify**

*Cleansing toxins and disease.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one creature. Remove one disease or poison condition affecting them.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Cleanse. Remove all diseases, poisons, and curses from one creature. They also heal 2d8 HP.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Greater Restoration. Remove all diseases, poisons, curses, and one of the following: Blinded, Deafened, Paralyzed, or reduced Attribute scores. Target heals to full HP.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

4. ### **4. Vital Surge**

*Flooding a creature with explosive life energy.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one creature. They gain 15 Temporary HP and +10 ft Speed for 1 minute.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Target gains 25 Temporary HP, +10 ft Speed, and Advantage on Vitality saves for 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Life Overflow. Target gains 40 Temporary HP, +20 ft Speed, Advantage on all saves, and gains Resistance to necrotic damage for 1 hour.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour**

5. ### **5. Commune with Nature**

*Speaking with plants and animals.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Speak with Animals. Communicate with beasts for 10 minutes. They are not compelled to help but are generally friendly unless threatened.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Speak with Plants. Communicate with plant life within 100 ft for 1 hour. Learn about recent events, creatures that passed by, weather patterns, etc.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Nature's Wisdom. Ask the natural world three questions about events within 1 mile in the past 24 hours. The GM must answer truthfully based on what nature "witnessed."**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Self / 100 ft / 1 mile**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / Instantaneous**

6. ### **6. Entangling Growth**

*Causing plants to restrain enemies.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target a 20 ft square within 60 ft. Vines, roots, and grass grow rapidly. The area becomes difficult terrain. Creatures in the area must make a Force Save (DC = 10 + Spirit Mod + Medicine Rank) or be Restrained. Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Thorned Vines. Area increases to 30 ft square. Restrained creatures take 2d6 piercing damage at the start of each turn. Duration: 10 minutes \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Living Jungle. Area increases to 40 ft square. The vines actively attack: make a melee attack (+Spirit Mod + Medicine Rank to hit) vs each creature in the area at the start of your turn (3d8 piercing damage on hit). Duration: 1 hour \[Concentration].**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Save: Force**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour \[Concentration]**

*Summoning a loyal beast ally.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**Summon a Large beast (bear, dire wolf, giant eagle) with AC 15, 60 HP, Speed 40 ft, and two attacks per turn (2d6 + 4 damage each). It can also use Help action as a bonus action. Duration: 8 hours.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**Primal Guardian. Summon a Huge beast (mammoth, tyrannosaurus, ancient tree-ent) with AC 17, 120 HP, Speed 50 ft. It makes two attacks per turn (3d8 + 5 damage each) and provides half cover to adjacent allies. Duration: 24 hours or permanent if you spend an additional 20 EP.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft (appears in unoccupied space)**  
**Duration: 1 hour / 8 hours / 24 hours or Permanent**

8. ### **8. Life Link**

*Binding life forces together.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one willing ally. For 10 minutes, whenever they receive healing, you heal for half that amount (and vice versa).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Life Transfer. Link with up to 3 allies within 30 ft. You can use your Reaction to redirect damage from a linked ally to yourself. Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Shared Vitality. Link up to 6 allies within 60 ft. All linked allies share a pool of 60 Temporary HP (damage to any ally reduces this pool). When anyone receives healing, all linked allies heal for Spirit Modifier HP. Duration: 1 hour.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch / 30 ft / 60 ft**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 1 hour**

*Your skin becomes like bark and stone.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Ironbark. Your DV becomes 18 and you gain Resistance to fire and cold damage. Duration: 8 hours.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Living Stone. Your DV becomes 20 and you gain Resistance to all physical damage (Bludgeoning, Piercing, Slashing). Any creature that hits you with a melee attack takes 1d8 piercing damage from thorns. Duration: 24 hours.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 1 hour / 8 hours / 24 hours**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Barrage of Thorns. Fire thorns at up to 5 different targets within 60 ft. Each takes 4d8 piercing damage (Force Save for half). Targets that fail are Poisoned and Restrained by thorny vines for 1 round.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Save: Vitality (T2-3), Force (T3)**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Create 10 enhanced berries. Each heals 1d8 HP and can be eaten as a Bonus Action.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Ambrosia. Create 5 golden fruits. Each heals 3d8 HP, removes one level of Exhaustion, and removes one disease or poison. Can be eaten as a Bonus Action.**|

**Action: Action**

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**9 EP**|**Apex Predator. Transform into a Huge beast or elemental creature. Gain Resistance to non-magical physical damage. You can cast Life Channeling Powers while transformed. Duration: 8 hours.**|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**True Resurrection. Touch a creature that died within the last 7 days. They return fully healed with all conditions, diseases, and curses removed. Their soul cannot refuse (even if trapped). You gain 2 levels of Exhaustion from this effort.**|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**9 EP**|**Earthquake. The ground shakes in a 60 ft radius centered on you. All creatures on the ground must make a Force Save or be knocked Prone and take 6d6 bludgeoning damage. Structures take double damage. The area becomes difficult terrain.**|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**Perfect Vitality. Create a 60 ft radius zone for 24 hours. All creatures inside heal 3d8 HP per turn, cannot die (automatically stabilize at 0 HP), and diseases/poisons are suppressed (return when leaving). Natural plants grow and flourish.**|

**Action: Action (Ritual - 10 minutes)**  
**Range: Self (centered)**  
**Duration: 1 hour / 8 hours / 24 hours \[Concentration]**

18. ## **14.5.H: Time Channeling (Spirit or Mind) - Time Realm**

*Drawing power from the Time Realm—temporal manipulation, precognition, chronal echoes, and the rewriting of causality.*

**Primary Attribute**: Spirit or Mind (Player's Choice)  
**Casting Skill**: Mysticism  
**Prerequisite**: Time Channeling Origin Tag + Mysticism Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Time Channeling connects you to the Time Realm, where past, present, and future flow like tributaries of the same river. These Powers allow you to manipulate the flow of time, see what has been and what will be, and rewrite moments of history. Masters of Time become temporal architects—beings who stand outside the normal flow of causality.

1. ### **1. Temporal Strike**

*Your attacks arrive before you make them.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Make one melee or ranged attack. The attack occurs simultaneously with your movement—you can attack a target, then move away without provoking opportunity attacks. Add +1d8 force damage.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Add +2d8 force damage. On hit, target loses their Reaction until the end of their next turn as they experience temporal displacement.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Add +4d8 force damage. On hit, target must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank) or skip their next turn entirely as they're frozen in time for 6 seconds.**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Melee or Ranged (Weapon Range)**  
**Save: Spirit (T3)**

2. ### **2. Accelerate**

*Speeding up an ally's personal timeline.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one ally within 30 ft. Their Speed doubles and they gain +2 to DV for 1 minute.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Haste. Target gains double Speed, +2 to DV, Advantage on Force saves, and one additional Action (Attack, Dash, Disengage, Hide, or Use Object only) on each of their turns. Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Rapid Time. Target immediately takes a full extra turn after your current turn ends. On that turn, they act as if 6 seconds have passed for them alone.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 1 minute \[Concentration] / Instantaneous**

3. ### **3. Slow**

*Trapping enemies in thick, syrupy time.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one creature within 60 ft. They must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank). On failure, their Speed is halved for 1 minute (save ends).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Time Drag. On failed save, target's Speed is halved, they cannot take Reactions, and can use either an Action or Bonus Action each turn (not both). Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Temporal Stasis. Target up to 3 creatures within 30 ft. Each must save or be Paralyzed in frozen time for 1 minute (save ends). They are aware but cannot move, speak, or perceive the passage of time.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft / 30 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 1 minute \[Concentration] / 1 minute**

4. ### **4. Foresight**

*Seeing attacks before they land.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Reaction. When targeted by an attack, add +1d6 to your DV against that attack.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Combat Precognition. For 1 minute, you cannot be Surprised, you have Advantage on Initiative rolls, and you have +3 to DV.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Perfect Dodge. For 10 minutes, once per round you can force one attack against you to automatically miss as you step out of the way before it arrives.**|

**Action: Reaction (T1) / Bonus Action (T2-3)**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 minute / 10 minutes**

5. ### **5. Rewind**

*Undoing recent events.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Reaction. When you or an ally within 30 ft fails a d20 roll, you can force a reroll. They must use the new result.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Temporal Reset. Reaction. When you or an ally within 30 ft takes damage, reduce that damage by 4d10 as you partially rewind the injury.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Undo. Reaction. When you or an ally within 60 ft would be reduced to 0 HP, they are instead restored to the HP they had at the start of their last turn. This can only affect each creature once per day.**|

**Action: Reaction**  
**Range: 30 ft / 60 ft**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

6. ### **6. Chronal Healing**

*Reversing injuries through time.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one creature. Heal them for 2d8 + Spirit/Mind Modifier HP as their wounds rewind.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Temporal Restoration. If cast within 1 round of a creature taking damage, heal them for 50% of the damage they just took (up to 6d8 maximum).**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Revert State. Touch one creature. They return to the exact HP and condition they had at the start of their last turn (up to 1 minute ago). All damage, conditions, and effects from that time are erased.**|

**Action: Action (T1, T3) / Reaction (T2)**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

7. ### **7. Age/Wither**

*Fast-forwarding entropy on targets.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Ranged attack against one creature within 60 ft. Deal 3d8 necrotic damage as they rapidly age.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 5d8 necrotic. Non-magical objects the target is holding age and deteriorate: metal weapons gain -2 to attack/damage, armor loses 2 AC (until repaired).**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Dust to Dust. Deal 8d8 necrotic damage. If this reduces the target to 0 HP, their body ages to dust instantly, requiring Tier 3 resurrection magic to restore. Constructs and objects are destroyed completely.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**

8. ### **8. Time Stop**

*Stepping outside the normal flow of moments.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**4 EP**|**Bonus Action. You gain one additional Action this turn (can be used for Movement, Dash, or Use Object only—not Attack).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**Brief Pause. Time stops for everyone except you for 1 round. You can move and take Actions, but attacking or damaging a creature immediately ends the effect.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**Temporal Mastery. Time stops for 1d4+1 rounds. You can move, use Powers, and interact with objects freely. Attacking ends the effect immediately. When time resumes, any delayed effects (thrown objects, prepared spells, etc.) occur simultaneously.**|

**Action: Bonus Action (T1) / Action (T2-3)**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 round / 1d4+1 rounds**

9. ### **9. Echo**

*Creating temporal duplicates.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Summon an echo of yourself from 6 seconds ago. It appears in an adjacent space, has 1 HP, and can make one melee attack (your weapon damage) before vanishing.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Temporal Clone. Summon an echo with HP equal to half your maximum. It lasts 1 minute and can act on your initiative, making attacks and moving. It shares your statistics but cannot use Powers.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Paradox Army. Summon up to 4 temporal echoes. Each has your full statistics and half your HP. They act on your initiative and last 1 minute. When they vanish, you gain 1 level of Exhaustion per echo summoned.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Adjacent space**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 minute / 1 minute**

10. ### **10. Glimpse the Future**

*Seeing probable outcomes.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Ask the GM if a specific action you're considering (e.g., "opening this door") will likely result in: Good Outcome, Bad Outcome, or Uncertain.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Divination. Ask the GM one yes/no question about events that will occur in the next hour. The GM must answer truthfully based on current trajectory.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Perfect Prophecy. Ask any question about events in the next 24 hours. The GM provides a detailed, accurate vision of the most likely outcome. You can act on this knowledge to change events.**|

**Action: Action (Ritual - 1 minute)**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

11. ### **11. Temporal Anchor**

*Preventing time manipulation.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one creature within 60 ft. For 1 minute, they cannot teleport, use time-manipulation Powers, or be affected by forced temporal effects (haste, slow, etc.).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Grounding. Target's Speed is reduced to 0 and they cannot take Reactions for 1 round. Flying creatures fall.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Temporal Prison. Target one creature within 30 ft. They must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank) or be placed in suspended animation for 1 hour. They are frozen in time—aware but unable to act, move, or be harmed.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30-60 ft**  
**Save: Spirit (T3)**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 1 round / 1 hour**

12. ### **12. Entropy Field**

*Creating a zone where time breaks down.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**4 EP**|**Create a 15 ft radius field within 60 ft. The area is difficult terrain and projectiles passing through have a 50% chance to miss. Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**6 EP**|**Time Distortion. Radius increases to 20 ft. Creatures starting their turn inside take 3d6 force damage as temporal stress tears at them. Duration: 10 minutes \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**9 EP**|**Causality Collapse. Radius increases to 30 ft. Objects inside rapidly age and crumble. Creatures take 5d6 force damage per round. Technology malfunctions. Magical effects flicker (50% chance to fail when cast). Duration: 1 hour \[Concentration].**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour \[Concentration]**

13. ### **13. Recall**

*Snapping targets back through time.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Reaction. When a creature within 30 ft moves, you can teleport them back to where they started their turn (Spirit Save negates, DC = 10 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Temporal Reversal. Target returns to their previous position and loses any healing or temporary effects gained this round.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Paradox Banishment. Target one creature within 60 ft. They must make a Spirit Save or be removed from the timeline for 1d4 rounds. They reappear in the same space (or nearest unoccupied space) with no memory of the missing time.**|

**Action: Reaction**  
**Range: 30 ft / 60 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1d4 rounds**

14. ### **14. Prescient Defense**

*Knowing exactly how to avoid harm.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Reaction. Add +1d6 to any d20 roll (yours or an ally's within 30 ft) before the result is determined.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Fated Outcome. Replace any d20 roll with a 10 (before seeing the result).**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Destiny Control. Replace any d20 roll with a 20 (automatic critical success). Can be used once per Long Rest.**|

**Action: Reaction**  
**Range: Self / 30 ft**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

15. ### **15. Temporal Loop**

*Trapping a moment to repeat endlessly.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**4 EP**|**Target one creature within 30 ft. They must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank) or repeat their last Action on their next turn (attacking same target, moving same direction, etc.).**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**Déjà Vu. Target repeats their entire last turn identically for 1 round (same movement, actions, targets). They are aware but cannot break the loop.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**Infinite Loop. Target is trapped repeating the same 6 seconds over and over for up to 1 minute \[Concentration]. To them, it feels like eternity. They are effectively Stunned, experiencing the same moment endlessly. When released, they must make a Spirit Save or gain 1 level of Exhaustion from the psychological trauma.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: 1 round / 1 round / 1 minute \[Concentration]**

19. ## **14.5.I: Sanctuary Channeling (Spirit) - Sanctuary Realm**

*Drawing power from the Sanctuary Realm—divine protection, sacred ground, refuge, and the preservation of the innocent.*

**Primary Attribute**: Spirit (Divine Connection/Protection)  
**Casting Skill**: Religion  
**Prerequisite**: Sanctuary Channeling Origin Tag + Religion Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Sanctuary Channeling connects you to the Sanctuary Realm, where all who seek refuge find protection and those who would do harm are turned away. These Powers allow you to create safe havens, shield allies from danger, and punish those who violate sacred peace. Masters of Sanctuary become living bastions—guardians who protect the weak and make any ground they stand upon holy.

1. ### **1. Guardian's Strike**

*Your attacks defend as they harm.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Make one melee or ranged attack. On hit, deal weapon damage +1d8 radiant damage. Until the start of your next turn, one ally within 10 ft of you gains +2 to DV.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Add +2d8 radiant damage. All allies within 10 ft gain +2 to DV until the start of your next turn.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Add +4d8 radiant damage. All allies within 20 ft gain +3 to DV and Resistance to the damage type your target deals (if known) for 1 minute.**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Melee or Ranged (Weapon Range)**  
**Duration: Until start of your next turn / 1 minute**

2. ### **2. Protective Ward**

*A shimmering barrier of divine light.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one ally within 30 ft. They gain 15 Temporary HP and +1 to DV for 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Target gains 25 Temporary HP, +2 to DV, and Resistance to necrotic and psychic damage for 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Divine Aegis. Target gains 40 Temporary HP, +3 to DV, and for the next minute, the first time they would take damage that would reduce them below 1 HP, they are instead reduced to 1 HP and the ward ends.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 1 minute**

3. ### **3. Sacred Ground**

*Blessing the earth beneath your feet.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**4 EP**|**Create a 20 ft radius zone centered on a point within 30 ft. Allies inside automatically stabilize when dying. Undead and fiends treat it as difficult terrain. Duration: 10 minutes \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**6 EP**|**Hallowed Ground. Radius increases to 30 ft. Enemies inside take 2d6 radiant damage at the start of their turns. Allies inside have Advantage on death saves. Duration: 1 hour \[Concentration].**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**10 EP**|**Perfect Sanctuary. Create a 60 ft radius zone. Enemies cannot willingly enter unless they succeed on a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Spirit Mod + Religion Rank). Those inside cannot attack or cast harmful Powers. Allies heal 2d8 HP at the start of their turns. Duration: 8 hours \[Concentration].**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30 ft (zone center)**  
**Save: Spirit (T3)**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 8 hours \[Concentration]**

*Divine protection surrounds an ally.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Target gains +3 to DV and Advantage on one type of save (Force, Vitality, Mind, or Spirit—your choice). Duration: 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Impenetrable Shield. Target gains +4 to DV and has Advantage on all saves for 1 hour. Once during the duration, they can use a Reaction to become immune to all damage from one attack.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: 30 ft**  
**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 hour / 1 hour**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Mass Restoration. Heal up to 6 creatures within 30 ft for 4d8 + Spirit Modifier HP each. All targets also remove one condition: Frightened, Poisoned, or Diseased.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft / 30 ft radius**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

*Forcing evil entities away.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one undead, fiend, or aberration within 30 ft. They must make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Spirit Mod + Religion Rank) or be Turned (they must use their movement to get as far from you as possible and cannot willingly move closer). Duration: 1 minute or until they take damage.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Radiant Rebuke. Affect all undead, fiends, and aberrations within 30 ft. Those that fail their save are Turned and take 3d8 radiant damage.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Divine Banishment. Target one extraplanar creature (fiend, celestial, elemental, etc.) within 60 ft. They must make a Spirit Save or be banished to their home plane for 1 minute. If they are native to another plane and fail by 5 or more, the banishment is permanent.**|

*Making a creature untouchable.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

Any enemy attempting to attack or target them with a harmful Power must first make a Spirit Save (DC = 10 + Spirit Mod + Religion Rank) or choose a different target. Duration: 1 minute. Ends if the protected creature attacks or casts a harmful Power.**|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Divine Invulnerability. For 1 round, target creature cannot be targeted by attacks or harmful effects of any kind. They are completely protected but can still act normally.**|

**Range: 30 ft**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Divine Sacrifice. You can take damage meant for up to 3 allies within 60 ft simultaneously. You gain Resistance to that damage.**|

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Miraculous Recovery. Heal target to full HP and remove all diseases, poisons, curses, and one of: Blinded, Deafened, Paralyzed, or Petrified. They also gain immunity to the removed condition for 24 hours.**|

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**The threshold increases to twice your level. The divine effect can be moderate (significant healing, removing curses, revealing information).**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**The threshold increases to three times your level. The divine effect can be powerful (resurrection, divine smiting of enemies, altering reality in a limited way). Even on failure, you can use it again after 24 hours.**|

Allies inside heal 2d8 HP per turn, have Advantage on all saves, and cannot be targeted by scrying or divination. The structure provides beds, food, and water for up to 12 people. Duration: 24 hours or permanent if you spend an additional 30 EP.**|

20. ## **14.5.J: Void Channeling (Spirit or Mind) - Void Realm**

*Drawing power from the Void Realm—entropy, anti-magic, emptiness, nullification, and the spaces between reality.*

**Primary Attribute**: Spirit or Mind (Player's Choice)  
**Casting Skill**: Mysticism  
**Prerequisite**: Void Channeling Origin Tag + Mysticism Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Void Channeling connects you to the Void Realm, where existence itself thins and entropy reigns supreme. These Powers allow you to unmake magic, drain life force, and harness the emptiness between all things. Masters of Void become walking nullification zones—entities that erase what should not be and consume all that stands before them.

1. ### **1. Void Bolt**

*A sphere of non-existence tears through matter.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Ranged attack against one creature within 60 ft. Deal 2d8 necrotic damage. If the target has Temporary HP, this attack deals double damage to the Temporary HP only.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 4d8 necrotic. On hit, target cannot regain HP until the start of your next turn as void energy clings to them.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Damage increases to 6d8 necrotic. If this reduces a creature to 0 HP, their body disintegrates into gray ash. Non-magical objects are completely destroyed. Resurrection requires Tier 3 magic.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**

2. ### **2. Nullify**

*Unraveling magical effects.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Target one active magical effect you can see within 60 ft. Make a Mysticism check (d20 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank) vs. the caster's Save DC. On success, the effect ends.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Counterspell. Reaction. When you see a creature within 60 ft cast a Power, you can attempt to counter it. Tier 1-2 Powers fail automatically. For Tier 3, make a Mysticism check (DC 15).**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Dead Magic Zone. Create a 30 ft radius sphere centered on a point within 60 ft. For 1 minute, no Powers or magical effects function within the zone. Existing effects are suppressed. Duration: 1 minute \[Concentration].**|

**Action: Action (T1, T3) / Reaction (T2)**  
**Range: 60 ft**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 minute \[Concentration]**

*Draining the life force of others.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Make a melee spell attack. On hit, deal 2d8 necrotic damage and heal yourself for half the damage dealt.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 4d8 necrotic. You heal for the full amount of damage dealt. Target's maximum HP is reduced by the damage dealt until they finish a Long Rest.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Life Drain. Damage increases to 6d8 necrotic. You heal for the full amount. Target must make a Vitality Save (DC = 10 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank) or gain one level of Exhaustion as their life force is drained away.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Melee (touch)**  
**Save: Vitality (T3)**

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**6 EP**|**Crushing Field. Radius increases to 20 ft. Creatures must make a Force Save (DC = 10 + Spirit/Mind Mod + Mysticism Rank) to leave the area. Damage increases to 4d6 force. Ranged attacks through the area automatically miss.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**9 EP**|**Singularity. Radius increases to 30 ft. At the start of each turn, all creatures in the area are pulled 20 ft toward the center. Those within 5 ft of the center take 8d6 force damage and are Restrained.**|

21. ## **14.5.K: Death Channeling (Vitality or Spirit) - Death Realm**

*Drawing power from the Death Realm—necromancy, decay, undead mastery, life's ending, and the cold embrace of mortality.*

**Primary Attribute**: Vitality or Spirit (Player's Choice)  
**Casting Skill**: Arcana  
**Prerequisite**: Death Channeling Origin Tag + Arcana Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Death Channeling connects you to the Death Realm, where all life ultimately ends and the boundary between living and dead grows thin. These Powers allow you to command undead, manipulate life force, and wield the inevitability of mortality as a weapon. Masters of Death become necromancers—lords who blur the line between life and death and command legions of the unliving.

1. ### **1. Death Bolt**

*A lance of pure necrotic energy.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Ranged attack against one creature within 60 ft. Deal 2d8 necrotic damage. Against undead, this instead heals them for 2d8 HP.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Damage increases to 4d8 necrotic. On hit, target's maximum HP is reduced by the damage dealt until they finish a Long Rest.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Death Ray. Damage increases to 6d8 necrotic. If this reduces a living creature to 0 HP, they die instantly and rise as a zombie under your control for 24 hours.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 60 ft**

2. ### **2. Raise Undead**

*Animating corpses to serve you.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**4 EP**|**Touch a humanoid corpse that has been dead for less than 30 days. It rises as a Skeleton or Zombie (CR 1/4) under your control. It has AC 13, 20 HP, and makes one attack per turn (1d6 + 2 damage). Duration: 8 hours.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**7 EP**|**You can raise up to 3 undead simultaneously. Each has AC 14, 30 HP, and makes one attack (2d6 + 3 damage). They follow complex commands and can operate independently. Duration: 24 hours.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**12 EP**|**Greater Undead. Raise a Ghoul, Wight, or Wraith (CR 3-5). It has all its normal abilities and statistics. You can maintain up to 5 undead servants simultaneously. Duration: Permanent until destroyed.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch**  
**Duration: 8 hours / 24 hours / Permanent**

*Siphoning vitality from the living.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one creature. Deal 2d8 necrotic damage. You heal HP equal to half the damage dealt.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Range increases to 30 ft. Damage increases to 4d8 necrotic. You heal HP equal to the full damage dealt.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Exsanguinate. Touch or target within 30 ft. Deal 6d8 necrotic damage. You heal the full amount. If the target is below half HP, they must make a Vitality Save (DC = 10 + Vitality/Spirit Mod + Arcana Rank) or be Stunned for 1 round as their life force is drained.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch / 30 ft**  
**Save: Vitality (T3)**

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Chain Reaction. You can detonate up to 3 corpses within range. Damage increases to 5d6 necrotic per explosion.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Living Bomb. Target one living creature below 50% HP within 30 ft. They must make a Vitality Save. On failure, they take 6d6 necrotic damage and explode. All creatures within 10 ft take the same damage. On success, they take half damage and don't explode.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: 30-60 ft**  
**Save: Force / Vitality (T3)**

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Ossified Shell. Gain 40 Temporary HP and +3 to DV. You gain Resistance to physical damage (Bludgeoning, Piercing, Slashing) while the Temporary HP lasts. Melee attackers take 2d8 piercing damage. Duration: 1 hour.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Séance. You can contact the spirit of a deceased person (not tied to a corpse) if you have a personal item of theirs. Ask up to 5 questions. Range: 1 mile.**|

When this ends, you gain 2 levels of Exhaustion.**|

22. ## **14.5.L: Liberation Channeling (Authority) - Liberation Realm**

*Drawing power from the Liberation Realm—freedom, breaking bonds, rebellion, escape, and the righteous defiance of tyranny.*

**Primary Attribute**: Authority (Social Power/Influence)  
**Casting Skill**: Persuasion  
**Prerequisite**: Liberation Channeling Origin Tag + Persuasion Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Liberation Channeling connects you to the Liberation Realm, where chains shatter and the oppressed rise against their oppressors. These Powers allow you to break restraints, inspire rebellion, and grant freedom to the downtrodden. Masters of Liberation become revolutionary leaders—champions who cannot be held and who lead others to throw off their shackles.

1. ### **1. Liberating Strike**

*Your attacks free as they harm.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Make one melee or ranged attack. On hit, deal weapon damage +1d8 force damage. You can immediately end one condition on yourself or an adjacent ally: Grappled, Restrained, or Frightened.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Add +2d8 force damage. On hit, you can end Grappled, Restrained, Frightened, or Charmed on yourself and all allies within 10 ft.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Add +4d8 force damage. On hit, you and all allies within 30 ft immediately end all restraining effects (Grappled, Restrained, Paralyzed, Petrified) and can move up to half their Speed without provoking opportunity attacks.**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Melee or Ranged (Weapon Range)**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

2. ### **2. Break Bonds**

*Shattering physical and magical restraints.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Touch one creature. End one of the following conditions: Grappled, Restrained, or Paralyzed. Mundane bonds (ropes, chains, manacles) break instantly.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Mass Freedom. All creatures you choose within 10 ft have Grappled, Restrained, and Paralyzed conditions removed. Magical restraints require an Authority check (d20 + Authority Mod + Persuasion Rank) vs. DC 15.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Absolute Liberation. All creatures within 30 ft (allies only) are freed from Grappled, Restrained, Paralyzed, Petrified, and Stunned conditions. All restraints (magical and mundane) shatter. Creatures can immediately move up to their Speed.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch / 10 ft / 30 ft**  
**Duration: Instantaneous**

3. ### **3. Unshackled Mind**

*Freeing minds from control.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**3 EP**|**Touch one creature. End one of the following conditions: Charmed, Frightened, or Dominated.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Mental Liberation. Target is freed from all Charm and Fear effects. They also gain Advantage on saves vs. mental control for 1 hour.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Shatter Control. All allies within 30 ft are freed from Charm, Fear, and Domination effects. They also become immune to these effects for 10 minutes. Creatures controlling them (if any) take 4d8 psychic damage.**|

**Action: Action**  
**Range: Touch / 30 ft**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 hour / 10 minutes**

*Slipping free from any imprisonment.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|**Tier 1**|**5 BP**|**2 EP**|**Bonus Action. You automatically escape from any non-magical restraints (ropes, chains, grapples). You can squeeze through spaces as narrow as 6 inches. Duration: 1 minute.**|
|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**4 EP**|**Perfect Escape. You can escape from magical restraints with an Authority check (d20 + Authority Mod + Persuasion Rank) vs. DC 15. You can move through enemy-occupied spaces without penalty. Duration: 10 minutes.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**7 EP**|**Ghost Step. For 1 hour, you can move through walls and barriers as if they weren't there (up to 5 ft thick). You automatically succeed on all checks to escape restraints. Opportunity attacks against you have Disadvantage.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**  
**Duration: 1 minute / 10 minutes / 1 hour**

*Igniting the fire of freedom in others.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Call to Arms. All allies within 60 ft immediately gain 15 Temporary HP, Advantage on attack rolls for 1 minute, and can use their Reaction to move up to their Speed and make one weapon attack.**|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: 30 ft / 60 ft**  
**Duration: Instantaneous / 1 minute**

*Opening routes of escape that shouldn't exist.*

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

**Duration: 10 minutes / 1 minute / Instantaneous**

|Tier|BP Cost|EP Cost|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|

**Action: Bonus Action**  
**Range: Self**

*Turning victims into fighters.*

|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**9 EP**|**Revolution. All enslaved, oppressed, or imprisoned beings within 100 ft (including those you can't see) are filled with revolutionary fervor. They immediately attempt to break free and fight their oppressors for 8 hours. The GM determines their capabilities.**|

12. ### **12. Untraceable**

*Becoming impossible to find or follow.*

|-|-|-|-|

23. ## **14.5.M: Dominion Channeling (Authority) - Dominion Realm**

*Drawing power from the Dominion Realm—control, command, authority, subjugation, and the absolute exercise of will over others.*

**Primary Attribute**: Authority (Social Power/Dominance)  
**Casting Skill**: Leadership  
**Prerequisite**: Dominion Channeling Origin Tag + Leadership Skill rank 1+

**Philosophy**: Dominion Channeling connects you to the Dominion Realm, where hierarchies are absolute and will is law. These Powers allow you to command obedience, dominate minds, and impose your authority on reality itself. Masters of Dominion become living tyrants—rulers whose word becomes reality and whose presence demands submission.

1. ### **1. Command Strike**

*Your attacks carry the weight of authority.*

|-|-|-|-|

|**Tier 2**|**+10 BP**|**5 EP**|**Add +2d8 psychic damage. You can issue a short sentence command (up to 10 words). Target has Disadvantage on the save.**|
|**Tier 3**|**+15 BP**|**8 EP**|**Add +4d8 psychic damage. You can issue a complex command. On a failed save, target obeys for 1 minute or until the command is fulfilled. Suicidal commands allow a new save each round.**|

**Action: Part of Attack Action**  
**Range: Melee or Ranged (Weapon Range)**  
**Save: Spirit**  
**Duration: 1 round / 1 minute**
